`server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap/entry.go`:
```go
// Package epmap maps anime episode numbers between AniDB and TVDB with the
// Anime-Lists anime-list-master.xml rules, and implements metadata.EpisodeMapper
// for absolute ↔ season numbering.
package epmap

import (
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// SeasonAbsolute marks a Target whose Episodes are TVDB absolute numbers
// (defaulttvdbseason="a" and no range named a season).
const SeasonAbsolute = -1

// Entry is one <anime> element of anime-list-master.xml.
type Entry struct {
	AniDBID       int
	Name          string
	TVDBID        int      // 0 when there is no TVDB series
	TVDBKind      string   // marker used instead of an id: "movie", "ova", "hentai", "web", "other", ""
	HasDefault    bool     // defaulttvdbseason was set
	Absolute      bool     // defaulttvdbseason="a"
	DefaultSeason int      // TVDB season of regular episodes (when HasDefault && !Absolute)
	EpisodeOffset int      // added to regular AniDB episode numbers
	TMDBTV        int      // tmdbtv
	TMDBMovieIDs  []int    // tmdbid (movies), comma-separated in the XML
	IMDBIDs       []string // imdbid, comma-separated in the XML
	Mappings      []Mapping
	EpisodeCount  int // regular episodes; 0 = unknown (filled by callers that know it)
}

// Mapping is one <mapping> element.
type Mapping struct {
	AniDBSeason int  // 1 regular, 0 specials
	TVDBSeason  int  // valid when HasTVDB
	HasTVDB     bool // false for TMDB-only mappings, which this package ignores
	Range       bool // start/end/offset rule (or a mapping without text)
	Start, End  int  // AniDB episode range, 0 = unbounded
	Offset      int
	Episodes    map[int][]int // explicit ";a-b;" pairs; {0} = no TVDB episode
}

// Target is where an AniDB episode lands on TVDB.
type Target struct {
	Season   int
	Episodes []int
}

// ParseEpisodeList parses the anime-lists text form ";1-5;2-6+7;3-0;".
func ParseEpisodeList(s string) (map[int][]int, error) {
	out := map[int][]int{}
	for _, part := range strings.Split(s, ";") {
		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
		if part == "" {
			continue
		}
		left, right, ok := strings.Cut(part, "-")
		if !ok {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("episode pair %q: missing '-'", part)
		}
		a, err := strconv.Atoi(left)
		if err != nil || a < 0 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("episode pair %q: bad AniDB episode", part)
		}
		var eps []int
		for _, r := range strings.Split(right, "+") {
			n, err := strconv.Atoi(r)
			if err != nil || n < 0 {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("episode pair %q: bad TVDB episode", part)
			}
			eps = append(eps, n)
		}
		out[a] = eps
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (m Mapping) contains(ep int) bool {
	return (m.Start == 0 || ep >= m.Start) && (m.End == 0 || ep <= m.End)
}

// explicit reports whether an explicit TVDB list names AniDB episode ep.
func (e Entry) explicit(anidbSeason, ep int) bool {
	for _, m := range e.Mappings {
		if m.HasTVDB && m.AniDBSeason == anidbSeason {
			if _, ok := m.Episodes[ep]; ok {
				return true
			}
		}
	}
	return false
}

// ToTVDB maps AniDB episode ep (anidbSeason 1 regular, 0 specials; trailers
// 200+n and "other" 400+n in season 0) of this entry to TVDB.
func (e Entry) ToTVDB(anidbSeason, ep int) (Target, bool) {
	if e.TVDBID == 0 || ep < 1 {
		return Target{}, false
	}
	for _, m := range e.Mappings {
		if !m.HasTVDB || m.AniDBSeason != anidbSeason {
			continue
		}
		if eps, ok := m.Episodes[ep]; ok {
			if len(eps) == 0 || (len(eps) == 1 && eps[0] == 0) {
				return Target{}, false
			}
			return Target{Season: m.TVDBSeason, Episodes: append([]int(nil), eps...)}, true
		}
	}
	if anidbSeason == 0 && ep > 200 {
		return Target{}, false // trailers and "other" only map through explicit lists
	}
	for _, m := range e.Mappings {
		if m.HasTVDB && m.Range && m.AniDBSeason == anidbSeason && m.contains(ep) && ep+m.Offset >= 1 {
			return Target{Season: m.TVDBSeason, Episodes: []int{ep + m.Offset}}, true
		}
	}
	if anidbSeason != 1 || !e.HasDefault || ep+e.EpisodeOffset < 1 {
		return Target{}, false
	}
	if e.Absolute {
		return Target{Season: SeasonAbsolute, Episodes: []int{ep + e.EpisodeOffset}}, true
	}
	return Target{Season: e.DefaultSeason, Episodes: []int{ep + e.EpisodeOffset}}, true
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap/series.go`:
```go
package epmap

import "sort"

// Series is every anime-lists entry that points at one TVDB series.
type Series []Entry

func (s Series) sorted() []Entry {
	out := append([]Entry(nil), s...)
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].AniDBID < out[j].AniDBID })
	return out
}

// Entry finds the entry with AniDB id aid.
func (s Series) Entry(aid int) (Entry, bool) {
	for _, e := range s {
		if e.AniDBID == aid {
			return e, true
		}
	}
	return Entry{}, false
}

// Primary is the entry that stands for the whole series: the absolute entry,
// else season 1 without offset, else any regular season, else season 0; ties
// go to the lowest AniDB id. Entries without a TVDB series never qualify.
func (s Series) Primary() (Entry, bool) {
	rank := func(e Entry) int {
		switch {
		case e.Absolute:
			return 0
		case e.HasDefault && e.DefaultSeason == 1 && e.EpisodeOffset == 0:
			return 1
		case e.HasDefault && e.DefaultSeason >= 1:
			return 2
		default:
			return 3
		}
	}
	var best Entry
	found := false
	for _, e := range s.sorted() {
		if e.TVDBID == 0 {
			continue
		}
		if !found || rank(e) < rank(best) {
			best, found = e, true
		}
	}
	return best, found
}

// FromTVDB finds the AniDB entry and episode for TVDB season/episode.
func (s Series) FromTVDB(season, ep int) (aid, anidbSeason, anidbEp int, ok bool) {
	entries := s.sorted()
	// 1. explicit pairs
	for _, e := range entries {
		for _, m := range e.Mappings {
			if !m.HasTVDB || m.TVDBSeason != season {
				continue
			}
			keys := make([]int, 0, len(m.Episodes))
			for a := range m.Episodes {
				keys = append(keys, a)
			}
			sort.Ints(keys)
			for _, a := range keys {
				for _, t := range m.Episodes[a] {
					if t == ep && t != 0 {
						return e.AniDBID, m.AniDBSeason, a, true
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	// 2. ranges
	for _, e := range entries {
		for _, m := range e.Mappings {
			if !m.HasTVDB || !m.Range || m.TVDBSeason != season {
				continue
			}
			a := ep - m.Offset
			if a >= 1 && m.contains(a) && !e.explicit(m.AniDBSeason, a) {
				return e.AniDBID, m.AniDBSeason, a, true
			}
		}
	}
	// 3. default seasons; the largest offset that still fits wins (cour splits)
	var best Entry
	found := false
	for _, e := range entries {
		if e.TVDBID == 0 || !e.HasDefault || e.Absolute || e.DefaultSeason != season {
			continue
		}
		a := ep - e.EpisodeOffset
		if a < 1 || (e.EpisodeCount > 0 && a > e.EpisodeCount) || e.explicit(1, a) {
			continue
		}
		if season == 0 && e.EpisodeCount == 0 {
			continue // season-0 defaults are only trusted with a known episode count
		}
		if !found || e.EpisodeOffset > best.EpisodeOffset {
			best, found = e, true
		}
	}
	if found {
		return best.AniDBID, 1, ep - best.EpisodeOffset, true
	}
	return 0, 0, 0, false
}

// AbsoluteToTVDB resolves a TVDB absolute number through an absolute entry's
// season ranges (e.g. One Piece).
func (s Series) AbsoluteToTVDB(abs int) (season, ep int, ok bool) {
	for _, e := range s.sorted() {
		if !e.Absolute || e.TVDBID == 0 {
			continue
		}
		if t, ok := e.ToTVDB(1, abs-e.EpisodeOffset); ok && t.Season != SeasonAbsolute {
			return t.Season, t.Episodes[0], true
		}
	}
	return 0, 0, false
}

// TVDBToAbsolute is the reverse of AbsoluteToTVDB.
func (s Series) TVDBToAbsolute(season, ep int) (int, bool) {
	aid, as, a, ok := s.FromTVDB(season, ep)
	if !ok || as != 1 {
		return 0, false
	}
	e, _ := s.Entry(aid)
	if !e.Absolute {
		return 0, false
	}
	return a + e.EpisodeOffset, true
}
```

`server/internal/db/migrations/20261025130100_anime_lists.sql`:
```sql
-- Anime-Lists/anime-lists anime-list-master.xml, one row per <anime>. entry is the
-- JSON form of epmap.Entry (mapping rules included). Replaced in full on refresh.
CREATE TABLE anime_lists_master (
    anidb_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    tvdb_id  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    entry    TEXT    NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE INDEX anime_lists_master_tvdb ON anime_lists_master(tvdb_id) WHERE tvdb_id > 0;

-- Fribb/anime-lists anime-list-full.json, one row per entry. NULL = unknown.
CREATE TABLE anime_ids (
    anidb_id    INTEGER,
    anilist_id  INTEGER,
    mal_id      INTEGER,
    tvdb_id     INTEGER,
    tvdb_season INTEGER,
    tvdb_offset INTEGER,
    tmdb_tv     INTEGER,
    tmdb_season INTEGER,
    tmdb_movie  INTEGER,
    imdb_id     TEXT,
    type        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
) STRICT;

CREATE INDEX anime_ids_anidb   ON anime_ids(anidb_id);
CREATE INDEX anime_ids_anilist ON anime_ids(anilist_id);
CREATE INDEX anime_ids_mal     ON anime_ids(mal_id);
CREATE INDEX anime_ids_tvdb    ON anime_ids(tvdb_id);
CREATE INDEX anime_ids_tmdb_tv ON anime_ids(tmdb_tv);
CREATE INDEX anime_ids_tmdb_mv ON anime_ids(tmdb_movie);
CREATE INDEX anime_ids_imdb    ON anime_ids(imdb_id);
```

```go
package animelists

import (
	"os"
	"reflect"
	"testing"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap"
)

func loadMaster(t *testing.T) map[int]epmap.Entry {
	t.Helper()
	f, err := os.Open("testdata/anime-list-master.xml")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer f.Close()
	entries, skipped, err := ParseMaster(f)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(entries) != 17 || skipped != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("entries=%d skipped=%d, want 17 and 2 (one bad mapping, one entry without id)", len(entries), skipped)
	}
	m := map[int]epmap.Entry{}
	for _, e := range entries {
		m[e.AniDBID] = e
	}
	return m
}

func TestParseMasterFields(t *testing.T) {
	m := loadMaster(t)

	op := m[69]
	if op.TVDBID != 81797 || !op.HasDefault || !op.Absolute || op.TMDBTV != 37854 || len(op.Mappings) != 8 {
		t.Fatalf("One Piece: %+v", op)
	}
	if op.Mappings[1].HasTVDB || !op.Mappings[1].Range {
		t.Fatalf("tmdb-only mapping must have HasTVDB=false, Range=true: %+v", op.Mappings[1])
	}
	if last := op.Mappings[7]; !last.HasTVDB || last.TVDBSeason != 23 || last.Start != 1156 || last.End != 0 || last.Offset != -1155 {
		t.Fatalf("open-ended range: %+v", last)
	}
	if sp := op.Mappings[0]; sp.Range || !reflect.DeepEqual(sp.Episodes[1], []int{27}) {
		t.Fatalf("explicit list: %+v", sp)
	}

	if p := m[8857]; p.TVDBID != 0 || p.TVDBKind != "movie" || !reflect.DeepEqual(p.TMDBMovieIDs, []int{212167}) || !reflect.DeepEqual(p.IMDBIDs, []string{"tt2981768"}) {
		t.Fatalf("Patema: %+v", p)
	}
	if e := m[12768]; e.TVDBID != 0 || e.TVDBKind != "" || e.HasDefault {
		t.Fatalf("unmapped entry: %+v", e)
	}
	if e := m[10583]; !reflect.DeepEqual(e.IMDBIDs, []string{"tt3646944", "tt3646946"}) {
		t.Fatalf("multiple imdb ids: %v", e.IMDBIDs)
	}
	if e := m[999999]; len(e.Mappings) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("broken mapping must be dropped, entry kept: %+v", e)
	}
	if e := m[5459]; !e.HasDefault || e.DefaultSeason != 0 || e.EpisodeOffset != 11 {
		t.Fatalf(".hack: %+v", e)
	}
}

// The parsed entries must give the same answers as the hand-built epmap fixtures.
func TestParsedEntriesMapEpisodes(t *testing.T) {
	m := loadMaster(t)
	cases := []struct {
		aid, anidbSeason, ep int
		season               int
		eps                  []int
	}{
		{69, 1, 100, 7, []int{9}},
		{69, 1, 1200, 23, []int{45}},
		{69, 0, 16, 0, []int{31}},
		{4880, 1, 222, 10, []int{26}},
		{14444, 1, 1, 3, []int{13}},
		{9826, 1, 4, 0, []int{15}},
		{10376, 0, 3, 0, []int{14}},
		{5459, 1, 1, 0, []int{12}},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		got, ok := m[c.aid].ToTVDB(c.anidbSeason, c.ep)
		if !ok || got.Season != c.season || !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Episodes, c.eps) {
			t.Errorf("aid %d %d/%d: got %+v ok=%v", c.aid, c.anidbSeason, c.ep, got, ok)
		}
	}
}

func TestParseFull(t *testing.T) {
	f, err := os.Open("testdata/anime-list-full.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer f.Close()
	rows, err := ParseFull(f)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(rows) != 11 {
		t.Fatalf("rows = %d, want 11 (the kitsu-only row is dropped)", len(rows))
	}
	by := map[int]IDRow{}
	for _, r := range rows {
		if r.AniDB != 0 {
			by[r.AniDB] = r
		}
	}
	want := IDRow{AniDB: 14444, AniList: 104578, MAL: 38524, TVDB: 267440, TVDBSeason: 3, TVDBOffset: 12,
		TMDBTV: 1429, TMDBSeason: 3, IMDB: "tt2560140", Type: "TV"}
	if by[14444] != want {
		t.Fatalf("14444 = %+v", by[14444])
	}
	if r := by[8857]; r.TMDBMovie != 212167 || r.TMDBTV != 0 || r.TVDBSeason != -1 || r.Type != "MOVIE" {
		t.Fatalf("movie row = %+v", r)
	}
	if r := by[69]; r.TVDBSeason != -1 || r.TMDBSeason != -1 {
		t.Fatalf("missing season must be -1: %+v", r)
	}
	if r := by[900002]; r.IMDB != "tt0900002" || r.TMDBMovie != 900002 || r.TMDBTV != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("legacy movie row = %+v", r)
	}
	if r := by[900003]; r.TVDB != 900003 || r.TMDBTV != 900004 {
		t.Fatalf("legacy tv row = %+v", r)
	}
}
```

```go
package animelists

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap"
)

// loadedStore returns a store filled from both fixtures.
func loadedStore(t *testing.T) *Store {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := NewStore(dbtest.Open(t))
	mf, _ := os.Open("testdata/anime-list-master.xml")
	defer mf.Close()
	entries, _, err := ParseMaster(mf)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.ReplaceMaster(ctx, entries); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ff, _ := os.Open("testdata/anime-list-full.json")
	defer ff.Close()
	rows, err := ParseFull(ff)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.ReplaceIDs(ctx, rows); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return s
}

func TestStoreEntries(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := loadedStore(t)

	e, ok, err := s.Entry(ctx, 14444)
	if err != nil || !ok || e.EpisodeOffset != 12 || e.DefaultSeason != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("Entry(14444) = %+v %v %v", e, ok, err)
	}
	if got, _ := e.ToTVDB(1, 1); got.Season != 3 || got.Episodes[0] != 13 {
		t.Fatalf("round-tripped entry lost its rules: %+v", got)
	}
	if _, ok, _ := s.Entry(ctx, 424242); ok {
		t.Fatal("unknown aid found")
	}
	series, err := s.EntriesByTVDB(ctx, 267440)
	if err != nil || len(series) != 9 {
		t.Fatalf("AoT series = %d entries, err %v", len(series), err)
	}
	if p, ok := series.Primary(); !ok || p.AniDBID != 9541 {
		t.Fatalf("primary = %d", p.AniDBID)
	}
	if aid, _, a, ok := series.FromTVDB(3, 13); !ok || aid != 14444 || a != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("FromTVDB(3,13) = %d/%d %v", aid, a, ok)
	}

	// Replace drops entries no longer in the file.
	if err := s.ReplaceMaster(ctx, []epmap.Entry{{AniDBID: 15, TVDBID: 79107, HasDefault: true, DefaultSeason: 1}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok, _ := s.Entry(ctx, 14444); ok {
		t.Fatal("stale entry survived ReplaceMaster")
	}
}

func TestStoreMediaType(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := loadedStore(t)
	for aid, want := range map[int]string{8857: "MOVIE", 9541: "TV", 424242: ""} {
		if got, err := s.MediaType(ctx, aid); err != nil || got != want {
			t.Errorf("MediaType(%d) = %q %v, want %q", aid, got, err, want)
		}
	}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists/parse.go`:
```go
// Package animelists loads the community anime ID datasets (Anime-Lists
// anime-list-master.xml and Fribb anime-list-full.json) and implements
// metadata.IDMapper.
package animelists

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"encoding/xml"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap"
)

type xmlAnime struct {
	AniDBID       string       `xml:"anidbid,attr"`
	TVDBID        string       `xml:"tvdbid,attr"`
	DefaultSeason string       `xml:"defaulttvdbseason,attr"`
	EpisodeOffset string       `xml:"episodeoffset,attr"`
	TMDBTV        string       `xml:"tmdbtv,attr"`
	TMDBID        string       `xml:"tmdbid,attr"`
	IMDBID        string       `xml:"imdbid,attr"`
	Name          string       `xml:"name"`
	Mappings      []xmlMapping `xml:"mapping-list>mapping"`
}

type xmlMapping struct {
	AniDBSeason string `xml:"anidbseason,attr"`
	TVDBSeason  string `xml:"tvdbseason,attr"`
	Start       string `xml:"start,attr"`
	End         string `xml:"end,attr"`
	Offset      string `xml:"offset,attr"`
	Text        string `xml:",chardata"`
}

// ParseMaster streams anime-list-master.xml. Entries without a valid AniDB id
// and mappings that cannot be parsed are skipped and counted.
func ParseMaster(r io.Reader) (entries []epmap.Entry, skipped int, err error) {
	dec := xml.NewDecoder(r)
	for {
		tok, err := dec.Token()
		if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
			return entries, skipped, nil
		}
		if err != nil {
			return nil, skipped, fmt.Errorf("anime-list-master: %w", err)
		}
		se, ok := tok.(xml.StartElement)
		if !ok || se.Name.Local != "anime" {
			continue
		}
		var x xmlAnime
		if err := dec.DecodeElement(&x, &se); err != nil {
			return nil, skipped, fmt.Errorf("anime-list-master: %w", err)
		}
		e, bad, ok := toEntry(x)
		skipped += bad
		if !ok {
			skipped++
			continue
		}
		entries = append(entries, e)
	}
}

// intAttr parses an optional integer attribute; "" gives 0.
func intAttr(s string) (int, bool) {
	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
	if s == "" {
		return 0, true
	}
	n, err := strconv.Atoi(s)
	return n, err == nil
}

func toEntry(x xmlAnime) (epmap.Entry, int, bool) {
	aid, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(x.AniDBID))
	if err != nil || aid <= 0 {
		return epmap.Entry{}, 0, false
	}
	e := epmap.Entry{AniDBID: aid, Name: strings.TrimSpace(x.Name)}
	tv := strings.TrimSpace(x.TVDBID)
	if n, err := strconv.Atoi(tv); err == nil && n > 0 {
		e.TVDBID = n
	} else {
		e.TVDBKind = strings.ToLower(tv)
	}
	switch ds := strings.TrimSpace(x.DefaultSeason); {
	case ds == "a":
		e.HasDefault, e.Absolute = true, true
	case ds != "":
		if n, err := strconv.Atoi(ds); err == nil && n >= 0 {
			e.HasDefault, e.DefaultSeason = true, n
		}
	}
	e.EpisodeOffset, _ = intAttr(x.EpisodeOffset)
	e.TMDBTV, _ = intAttr(x.TMDBTV)
	for _, p := range strings.Split(x.TMDBID, ",") {
		if n, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(p)); err == nil && n > 0 {
			e.TMDBMovieIDs = append(e.TMDBMovieIDs, n)
		}
	}
	for _, p := range strings.Split(x.IMDBID, ",") {
		if p = strings.TrimSpace(p); strings.HasPrefix(p, "tt") {
			e.IMDBIDs = append(e.IMDBIDs, p)
		}
	}
	bad := 0
	for _, xm := range x.Mappings {
		m, ok := toMapping(xm)
		if !ok {
			bad++
			continue
		}
		e.Mappings = append(e.Mappings, m)
	}
	return e, bad, true
}

func toMapping(x xmlMapping) (epmap.Mapping, bool) {
	as, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(x.AniDBSeason))
	if err != nil {
		return epmap.Mapping{}, false
	}
	m := epmap.Mapping{AniDBSeason: as}
	if ts := strings.TrimSpace(x.TVDBSeason); ts != "" {
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(ts)
		if err != nil {
			return epmap.Mapping{}, false
		}
		m.TVDBSeason, m.HasTVDB = n, true
	}
	var ok1, ok2, ok3 bool
	m.Start, ok1 = intAttr(x.Start)
	m.End, ok2 = intAttr(x.End)
	m.Offset, ok3 = intAttr(x.Offset)
	if !ok1 || !ok2 || !ok3 {
		return epmap.Mapping{}, false
	}
	text := strings.TrimSpace(x.Text)
	if text != "" {
		eps, err := epmap.ParseEpisodeList(text)
		if err != nil {
			return epmap.Mapping{}, false
		}
		m.Episodes = eps
	}
	m.Range = text == "" || strings.TrimSpace(x.Start) != "" || strings.TrimSpace(x.End) != "" || strings.TrimSpace(x.Offset) != ""
	return m, true
}

// IDRow is one entry of anime-list-full.json. Seasons are -1 when unknown.
type IDRow struct {
	AniDB, AniList, MAL, TVDB int
	TVDBSeason, TVDBOffset    int
	TMDBTV, TMDBSeason        int
	TMDBMovie                 int
	IMDB                      string
	Type                      string
}

type fullRow struct {
	Type          string          `json:"type"`
	AniDB         int             `json:"anidb_id"`
	AniList       int             `json:"anilist_id"`
	MAL           int             `json:"mal_id"`
	TVDB          int             `json:"tvdb_id"`
	TheTVDB       int             `json:"thetvdb_id"`
	IMDB          json.RawMessage `json:"imdb_id"`
	TMDB          json.RawMessage `json:"themoviedb_id"`
	Season        *struct {
		TVDB *int `json:"tvdb"`
		TMDB *int `json:"tmdb"`
	} `json:"season"`
	EpisodeOffset *struct {
		TVDB int `json:"tvdb"`
	} `json:"episode_offset"`
}

// ParseFull reads anime-list-full.json. Rows without an AniDB, AniList or MAL
// id are dropped (they cannot be pivoted on).
func ParseFull(r io.Reader) ([]IDRow, error) {
	var raw []fullRow
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r).Decode(&raw); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anime-list-full: %w", err)
	}
	rows := make([]IDRow, 0, len(raw))
	for _, f := range raw {
		if f.AniDB == 0 && f.AniList == 0 && f.MAL == 0 {
			continue
		}
		row := IDRow{AniDB: f.AniDB, AniList: f.AniList, MAL: f.MAL, TVDB: f.TVDB,
			TVDBSeason: -1, TMDBSeason: -1, Type: strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(f.Type))}
		if row.TVDB == 0 {
			row.TVDB = f.TheTVDB
		}
		row.IMDB = firstString(f.IMDB)
		row.TMDBTV, row.TMDBMovie = tmdbIDs(f.TMDB, row.Type)
		if f.Season != nil {
			if f.Season.TVDB != nil {
				row.TVDBSeason = *f.Season.TVDB
			}
			if f.Season.TMDB != nil {
				row.TMDBSeason = *f.Season.TMDB
			}
		}
		if f.EpisodeOffset != nil {
			row.TVDBOffset = f.EpisodeOffset.TVDB
		}
		rows = append(rows, row)
	}
	return rows, nil
}

func firstString(raw json.RawMessage) string {
	var s string
	if json.Unmarshal(raw, &s) == nil {
		return s
	}
	var list []string
	if json.Unmarshal(raw, &list) == nil && len(list) > 0 {
		return list[0]
	}
	return ""
}

func firstInt(raw json.RawMessage) int {
	var n int
	if json.Unmarshal(raw, &n) == nil {
		return n
	}
	var list []int
	if json.Unmarshal(raw, &list) == nil && len(list) > 0 {
		return list[0]
	}
	return 0
}

// tmdbIDs reads {"tv": n} / {"movie": [n]} or the older bare number (a movie id
// for MOVIE rows, a TV id otherwise).
func tmdbIDs(raw json.RawMessage, typ string) (tv, movie int) {
	var n int
	if json.Unmarshal(raw, &n) == nil {
		if n <= 0 {
			return 0, 0
		}
		if typ == "MOVIE" {
			return 0, n
		}
		return n, 0
	}
	var obj struct {
		TV    json.RawMessage `json:"tv"`
		Movie json.RawMessage `json:"movie"`
	}
	if json.Unmarshal(raw, &obj) != nil {
		return 0, 0
	}
	return firstInt(obj.TV), firstInt(obj.Movie)
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists/store.go`:
```go
package animelists

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap"
)

// Store owns anime_lists_master and anime_ids.
type Store struct{ db *db.DB }

func NewStore(d *db.DB) *Store { return &Store{db: d} }

// ReplaceMaster swaps the whole anime-list-master table in one transaction.
func (s *Store) ReplaceMaster(ctx context.Context, entries []epmap.Entry) error {
	tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer tx.Rollback()
	if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM anime_lists_master`); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("clear anime_lists_master: %w", err)
	}
	stmt, err := tx.PrepareContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO anime_lists_master(anidb_id, tvdb_id, entry) VALUES(?, ?, ?)
		ON CONFLICT(anidb_id) DO UPDATE SET tvdb_id = excluded.tvdb_id, entry = excluded.entry`)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer stmt.Close()
	for _, e := range entries {
		b, err := json.Marshal(e)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := stmt.ExecContext(ctx, e.AniDBID, e.TVDBID, string(b)); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("insert anime-list entry %d: %w", e.AniDBID, err)
		}
	}
	return tx.Commit()
}

func nz(n int) any {
	if n == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	return n
}

func nseason(n int) any {
	if n < 0 {
		return nil
	}
	return n
}

func nstr(s string) any {
	if s == "" {
		return nil
	}
	return s
}

// ReplaceIDs swaps the whole anime_ids table in one transaction.
func (s *Store) ReplaceIDs(ctx context.Context, rows []IDRow) error {
	tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer tx.Rollback()
	if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM anime_ids`); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("clear anime_ids: %w", err)
	}
	stmt, err := tx.PrepareContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO anime_ids(anidb_id, anilist_id, mal_id, tvdb_id,
		tvdb_season, tvdb_offset, tmdb_tv, tmdb_season, tmdb_movie, imdb_id, type)
		VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer stmt.Close()
	for _, r := range rows {
		if _, err := stmt.ExecContext(ctx, nz(r.AniDB), nz(r.AniList), nz(r.MAL), nz(r.TVDB),
			nseason(r.TVDBSeason), r.TVDBOffset, nz(r.TMDBTV), nseason(r.TMDBSeason), nz(r.TMDBMovie),
			nstr(r.IMDB), r.Type); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("insert anime ids: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return tx.Commit()
}

// Entry returns the anime-list-master entry for an AniDB id.
func (s *Store) Entry(ctx context.Context, aid int) (epmap.Entry, bool, error) {
	var raw string
	err := s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT entry FROM anime_lists_master WHERE anidb_id = ?`, aid).Scan(&raw)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return epmap.Entry{}, false, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return epmap.Entry{}, false, fmt.Errorf("anime-list entry %d: %w", aid, err)
	}
	var e epmap.Entry
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &e); err != nil {
		return epmap.Entry{}, false, fmt.Errorf("anime-list entry %d: %w", aid, err)
	}
	return e, true, nil
}

// EntriesByTVDB returns every entry pointing at one TVDB series.
func (s *Store) EntriesByTVDB(ctx context.Context, tvdbID int) (epmap.Series, error) {
	rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, `SELECT entry FROM anime_lists_master WHERE tvdb_id = ? AND tvdb_id > 0 ORDER BY anidb_id`, tvdbID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anime-list entries for tvdb %d: %w", tvdbID, err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	var out epmap.Series
	for rows.Next() {
		var raw string
		if err := rows.Scan(&raw); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		var e epmap.Entry
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &e); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out = append(out, e)
	}
	return out, rows.Err()
}

// MediaType is Fribb's "type" for an AniDB id ("TV", "MOVIE", "OVA", ...), or "".
func (s *Store) MediaType(ctx context.Context, aid int) (string, error) {
	var t string
	err := s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT type FROM anime_ids WHERE anidb_id = ? LIMIT 1`, aid).Scan(&t)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return "", nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("anime type %d: %w", aid, err)
	}
	return t, nil
}
```

---

### Task 6: ID cross-reference (`IDMapper.Expand`) and dataset definitions

`Expand` never overwrites an ID the caller already has. It pivots on the most specific ID first:
`anidb`, `anilist`, `mal` (one row each), then `tvdb` (via the anime-lists series' **primary**
entry, because one TVDB series has many AniDB entries), then `tmdb` (TV first, then movie), then
`imdb`. When several Fribb rows share a TVDB/TMDB/IMDb id (AoT seasons all share
`tt2560140`), the row for season 1 without offset wins. After the Fribb row, the
anime-list-master entry for the AniDB id fills `tvdb`, `tmdb` and `imdb` if still missing.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists/expand.go`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists/expand_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Store` (Task 5), `anime.IDInt`, `anime.SetID`, `animecache.Dataset`, `metadata.IDMapper`.
- Produces:
  - `(*Store).Expand(ctx context.Context, ids map[string]string) (map[string]string, error)` (implements `metadata.IDMapper`; returns a new map)
  - `animelists.MasterURL`, `animelists.FullURL` (production URLs)
  - `(*Store).Datasets(masterURL, fullURL string) []animecache.Dataset` — names
    `anime-lists-master` (file `anime-list-master.xml`) and `anime-lists-ids` (file
    `anime-list-full.json`), TTL 7 days, MinRetry 6 h; a load with zero entries is an error.

```go
package animelists

import (

	"testing"

)

func TestExpand(t *testing.T) {
	s := loadedStore(t)
	aot3b := map[string]string{"anidb": "14444", "anilist": "104578", "mal": "38524", "tvdb": "267440", "tmdb": "1429", "imdb": "tt2560140"}
	aot1 := map[string]string{"anidb": "9541", "anilist": "16498", "mal": "16498", "tvdb": "267440", "tmdb": "1429", "imdb": "tt2560140"}
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		in   map[string]string
		want map[string]string
	}{
		{"from anilist", map[string]string{"anilist": "104578"}, aot3b},
		{"from mal", map[string]string{"mal": "38524"}, aot3b},
		{"from anidb", map[string]string{"anidb": "14444"}, aot3b},
		{"from tvdb picks the series' primary entry", map[string]string{"tvdb": "267440"}, aot1},
		{"from tmdb tv picks season 1", map[string]string{"tmdb": "1429"}, aot1},
		{"from imdb shared by seasons picks season 1", map[string]string{"imdb": "tt2560140"}, aot1},
		{"absolute series from tvdb", map[string]string{"tvdb": "81797"},
			map[string]string{"anidb": "69", "anilist": "21", "mal": "21", "tvdb": "81797", "tmdb": "37854", "imdb": "tt0388629"}},
		{"movie from tmdb movie id", map[string]string{"tmdb": "212167"},
			map[string]string{"anidb": "8857", "anilist": "12477", "mal": "12477", "tmdb": "212167", "imdb": "tt2981768"}},
		{"master fills ids missing in Fribb", map[string]string{"anidb": "10376"},
			map[string]string{"anidb": "10376", "tvdb": "259640", "tmdb": "45782"}},
		{"known ids are never overwritten", map[string]string{"anidb": "9541", "tvdb": "999"},
			map[string]string{"anidb": "9541", "anilist": "16498", "mal": "16498", "tvdb": "999", "tmdb": "1429", "imdb": "tt2560140"}},
		{"unknown id unchanged", map[string]string{"mal": "424242"}, map[string]string{"mal": "424242"}},
		{"empty", map[string]string{}, map[string]string{}},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			in := map[string]string{}
			for k, v := range c.in {
				in[k] = v
			}
			got, err := s.Expand(context.Background(), in)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, c.want) {
				t.Fatalf("Expand(%v) = %v, want %v", c.in, got, c.want)
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(in, c.in) {
				t.Fatalf("input map was modified: %v", in)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestDatasetsLoadFixtures(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc("/master.xml", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		http.ServeFile(w, r, "testdata/anime-list-master.xml")
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("/full.json", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		http.ServeFile(w, r, "testdata/anime-list-full.json")
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("/empty.xml", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		io.WriteString(w, `<?xml version="1.0"?><anime-list></anime-list>`)
	})
	srv := httptest.NewServer(mux)
	defer srv.Close()

	d := dbtest.Open(t)
	s := NewStore(d)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	cl := clock.Client(0)
	cl.Retries = 0
	ds := &animecache.Datasets{DB: d, Client: cl, Dir: t.TempDir(), Now: clock.Now,
		Log: slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))}

	sets := s.Datasets(srv.URL+"/master.xml", srv.URL+"/full.json")
	if len(sets) != 2 || sets[0].Name != "anime-lists-master" || sets[1].Name != "anime-lists-ids" {
		t.Fatalf("datasets %+v", sets)
	}
	for _, set := range sets {
		if loaded, err := ds.Ensure(ctx, set); err != nil || !loaded {
			t.Fatalf("%s: loaded=%v err=%v", set.Name, loaded, err)
		}
	}
	if _, ok, _ := s.Entry(ctx, 14444); !ok {
		t.Fatal("master not loaded")
	}
	if typ, _ := s.MediaType(ctx, 8857); typ != "MOVIE" {
		t.Fatal("ids not loaded")
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(ds.Dir + "/anime-list-master.xml"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("file copy: %v", err)
	}

	empty := s.Datasets(srv.URL+"/empty.xml", srv.URL+"/full.json")[0]
	empty.Name, empty.File = "empty-master", "empty.xml" // own file name: no fresh copy to fall back on
	if _, err := ds.Ensure(ctx, empty); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an empty list must not replace the stored one")
	}
	if _, ok, _ := s.Entry(ctx, 14444); !ok {
		t.Fatal("stored entries were wiped by an empty download")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists/expand.go`:
```go
package animelists

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
)

// Production dataset URLs (raw GitHub files of the two community projects).
const (
	MasterURL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Anime-Lists/anime-lists/master/anime-list-master.xml"
	FullURL   = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Fribb/anime-lists/master/anime-list-full.json"
)

var _ metadata.IDMapper = (*Store)(nil)

// Expand fills every ID it can find from any one known ID. Existing keys are
// never changed. Keys: anidb, anilist, mal, tvdb, tmdb, imdb.
func (s *Store) Expand(ctx context.Context, ids map[string]string) (map[string]string, error) {
	out := make(map[string]string, len(ids)+6)
	for k, v := range ids {
		out[k] = v
	}
	row, ok, err := s.pivot(ctx, out)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if ok {
		anime.SetID(out, "anidb", row.AniDB)
		anime.SetID(out, "anilist", row.AniList)
		anime.SetID(out, "mal", row.MAL)
		anime.SetID(out, "tvdb", row.TVDB)
		if row.TMDBTV > 0 {
			anime.SetID(out, "tmdb", row.TMDBTV)
		} else {
			anime.SetID(out, "tmdb", row.TMDBMovie)
		}
		if row.IMDB != "" && out["imdb"] == "" {
			out["imdb"] = row.IMDB
		}
	}
	if aid, ok := anime.IDInt(out, "anidb"); ok {
		e, found, err := s.Entry(ctx, aid)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if found {
			anime.SetID(out, "tvdb", e.TVDBID)
			if e.TMDBTV > 0 {
				anime.SetID(out, "tmdb", e.TMDBTV)
			} else if len(e.TMDBMovieIDs) > 0 {
				anime.SetID(out, "tmdb", e.TMDBMovieIDs[0])
			}
			if len(e.IMDBIDs) > 0 && out["imdb"] == "" {
				out["imdb"] = e.IMDBIDs[0]
			}
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *Store) pivot(ctx context.Context, ids map[string]string) (IDRow, bool, error) {
	for _, p := range []struct{ key, where string }{
		{"anidb", "anidb_id = ?"}, {"anilist", "anilist_id = ?"}, {"mal", "mal_id = ?"},
	} {
		if v, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, p.key); ok {
			if row, found, err := s.rowWhere(ctx, p.where, v); err != nil || found {
				return row, found, err
			}
		}
	}
	if tvdb, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "tvdb"); ok {
		series, err := s.EntriesByTVDB(ctx, tvdb)
		if err != nil {
			return IDRow{}, false, err
		}
		if e, ok := series.Primary(); ok {
			row, found, err := s.rowWhere(ctx, "anidb_id = ?", e.AniDBID)
			if err != nil || found {
				return row, found, err
			}
			return IDRow{AniDB: e.AniDBID, TVDB: tvdb, TVDBSeason: -1, TMDBSeason: -1}, true, nil
		}
		if row, found, err := s.rowWhere(ctx, "tvdb_id = ?", tvdb); err != nil || found {
			return row, found, err
		}
	}
	if tmdb, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "tmdb"); ok {
		if row, found, err := s.rowWhere(ctx, "tmdb_tv = ?", tmdb); err != nil || found {
			return row, found, err
		}
		if row, found, err := s.rowWhere(ctx, "tmdb_movie = ?", tmdb); err != nil || found {
			return row, found, err
		}
	}
	if imdb := strings.TrimSpace(ids["imdb"]); imdb != "" {
		return s.rowWhere(ctx, "imdb_id = ?", imdb)
	}
	return IDRow{}, false, nil
}

// rowOrder prefers season 1 without offset when an id is shared by several rows.
const rowOrder = ` ORDER BY (tvdb_season = 1) DESC, COALESCE(tvdb_offset, 0), (tmdb_season = 1) DESC,
	COALESCE(anidb_id, 2147483647), COALESCE(anilist_id, 2147483647) LIMIT 1`

// rowWhere runs a fixed WHERE clause (never user text) with one argument.
func (s *Store) rowWhere(ctx context.Context, where string, arg any) (IDRow, bool, error) {
	var anidb, anilist, mal, tvdb, tvdbSeason, tvdbOffset, tmdbTV, tmdbSeason, tmdbMovie sql.NullInt64
	var imdb sql.NullString
	var r IDRow
	err := s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT anidb_id, anilist_id, mal_id, tvdb_id, tvdb_season, tvdb_offset,
		tmdb_tv, tmdb_season, tmdb_movie, imdb_id, type FROM anime_ids WHERE `+where+rowOrder, arg).
		Scan(&anidb, &anilist, &mal, &tvdb, &tvdbSeason, &tvdbOffset, &tmdbTV, &tmdbSeason, &tmdbMovie, &imdb, &r.Type)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return IDRow{}, false, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return IDRow{}, false, fmt.Errorf("anime ids lookup: %w", err)
	}
	r.AniDB, r.AniList, r.MAL, r.TVDB = int(anidb.Int64), int(anilist.Int64), int(mal.Int64), int(tvdb.Int64)
	r.TVDBSeason, r.TMDBSeason = -1, -1
	if tvdbSeason.Valid {
		r.TVDBSeason = int(tvdbSeason.Int64)
	}
	if tmdbSeason.Valid {
		r.TMDBSeason = int(tmdbSeason.Int64)
	}
	r.TVDBOffset, r.TMDBTV, r.TMDBMovie, r.IMDB = int(tvdbOffset.Int64), int(tmdbTV.Int64), int(tmdbMovie.Int64), imdb.String
	return r, true, nil
}

// Datasets returns the two weekly community datasets bound to this store.
func (s *Store) Datasets(masterURL, fullURL string) []animecache.Dataset {
	return []animecache.Dataset{
		{
			Name: "anime-lists-master", URL: masterURL, File: "anime-list-master.xml",
			TTL: 7 * 24 * time.Hour, MinRetry: 6 * time.Hour,
			Load: func(ctx context.Context, body []byte) (int, error) {
				entries, _, err := ParseMaster(bytes.NewReader(body))
				if err != nil {
					return 0, err
				}
				if len(entries) == 0 {
					return 0, errors.New("anime-list-master: no entries")
				}
				return len(entries), s.ReplaceMaster(ctx, entries)
			},
		},
		{
			Name: "anime-lists-ids", URL: fullURL, File: "anime-list-full.json",
			TTL: 7 * 24 * time.Hour, MinRetry: 6 * time.Hour,
			Load: func(ctx context.Context, body []byte) (int, error) {
				rows, err := ParseFull(bytes.NewReader(body))
				if err != nil {
					return 0, err
				}
				if len(rows) == 0 {
					return 0, errors.New("anime-list-full: no rows")
				}
				return len(rows), s.ReplaceIDs(ctx, rows)
			},
		},
	}
}
```

---

### Task 7: Episode mapper (`metadata.EpisodeMapper`)

How 1C numbers anime files (from the 1C plan): in `season` libraries `SxxEyy` is kept and
absolute-only files go into the folder's season (or 1) with `episode = absolute`; in `absolute`
libraries every non-special episode is season 1 with `episode = absolute`. Specials stay season 0.
`AbsoluteNumber` is stored whenever the file name has it. So the mapper must cope with
"S03E50 (absolute 50)", "S01E1094", and correct "S03E13 (absolute 50)" alike.

`Mapper.Map(show, ep)` returns the **TVDB-layout** season/episode of `show.Seasons` plus the
absolute number:
1. Season 0 → passed through (`absolute` nil).
2. Absolute input → converted. Absolute input is `AbsoluteNumber`, or in `absolute` libraries the
   episode number of a season-1 (or seasonless) file. It is used directly in `absolute` libraries
   and when the file has no season/episode.
3. `season` libraries: `(s, e)` present in the layout → kept (absolute from the layout). Otherwise
   try, in order: `S01Exxx` beyond the end of season 1 as an absolute number; `AbsoluteNumber`;
   pass `(s, e)` through (absolute from anime-lists ranges when the series is absolute, else nil).
4. Absolute → season/episode uses the record's layout first (absolute numbers from the provider,
   or counted across regular seasons when no episode has one), then the anime-lists absolute
   entry's TVDB ranges (works for One Piece even before the record has episodes).
5. `ok == false` only when nothing usable can be produced.

Multi-episode files (`EpisodeNumberEnd`) map by their first episode; the registry handles the range.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap/mapper.go`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap/mapper_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `metadata.Record`, `metadata.SeasonRecord`, `metadata.EpisodeRecord`, `metadata.EpisodeMapper`,
  `media.Item`, `library.Library` (`Options.AnimeNumbering`), `anime.IDInt`, `Series` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `type epmap.Lookup interface{ EntriesByTVDB(ctx context.Context, tvdbID int) (Series, error) }` (implemented by `*animelists.Store`)
  - `type epmap.Libraries interface{ Get(ctx context.Context, id int64) (library.Library, error) }` (implemented by `*library.Store`)
  - `epmap.NewMapper(l Lookup, libs Libraries, log *slog.Logger) *Mapper`
  - `(*Mapper).Map(ctx, show metadata.Record, ep media.Item) (season, episode int, absolute *int, ok bool)`

`server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap/mapper_test.go`:
```go
package epmap

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"testing"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/media"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
)

const (
	libSeason   int64 = 1
	libAbsolute int64 = 2
	libBroken   int64 = 3
)

type fakeLibs map[int64]library.Library

func (f fakeLibs) Get(_ context.Context, id int64) (library.Library, error) {
	if l, ok := f[id]; ok {
		return l, nil
	}
	return library.Library{}, errors.New("no such library")
}

type fakeLookup map[int]Series

func (f fakeLookup) EntriesByTVDB(_ context.Context, tvdb int) (Series, error) { return f[tvdb], nil }

func ip(n int) *int { return &n }

// seasons builds a TVDB-layout record: counts[season] regular episodes, plus
// specials S0E1..S0E10. withAbs sets Absolute like the AniDB provider does.
func seasons(counts []int, withAbs bool) []metadata.SeasonRecord {
	out := []metadata.SeasonRecord{{Number: 0}}
	for e := 1; e <= 10; e++ {
		out[0].Episodes = append(out[0].Episodes, metadata.EpisodeRecord{Season: 0, Number: e})
	}
	abs := 0
	for i, n := range counts {
		s := metadata.SeasonRecord{Number: i + 1}
		for e := 1; e <= n; e++ {
			abs++
			ep := metadata.EpisodeRecord{Season: i + 1, Number: e}
			if withAbs {
				ep.Absolute = ip(abs)
			}
			s.Episodes = append(s.Episodes, ep)
		}
		out = append(out, s)
	}
	return out
}

func newTestMapper() *Mapper {
	libs := fakeLibs{
		libSeason:   {ID: libSeason, Type: library.Type("anime"), Options: library.Options{AnimeNumbering: "season"}},
		libAbsolute: {ID: libAbsolute, Type: library.Type("anime"), Options: library.Options{AnimeNumbering: "absolute"}},
	}
	lookup := fakeLookup{81797: Series{onePiece}, 267440: aotSeries}
	return NewMapper(lookup, libs, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
}

func TestMap(t *testing.T) {
	// Attack on Titan on TVDB: S1 25, S2 12, S3 22, S4 30 → absolute 1-25, 26-37, 38-59, 60-89.
	aotIDs := map[string]string{"tvdb": "267440", "anidb": "9541"}
	aot := metadata.Record{IDs: aotIDs, Seasons: seasons([]int{25, 12, 22, 30}, true)}
	aotCounted := metadata.Record{IDs: aotIDs, Seasons: seasons([]int{25, 12, 22, 30}, false)}
	// Provider knew absolute numbers only up to S2 (a later AniDB entry was unavailable).
	aotPartial := metadata.Record{IDs: aotIDs, Seasons: seasons([]int{25, 12, 22}, true)}
	for i := range aotPartial.Seasons[3].Episodes {
		aotPartial.Seasons[3].Episodes[i].Absolute = nil
	}
	// One Piece before any episode list arrived: only IDs.
	op := metadata.Record{IDs: map[string]string{"tvdb": "81797", "anidb": "69"}}
	noIDs := metadata.Record{}

	type want struct {
		season, episode int
		abs             *int
		ok              bool
	}
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		show metadata.Record
		item media.Item
		want want
	}{
		{"season lib: SxxEyy kept with absolute",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(3), EpisodeNumber: ip(13)}, want{3, 13, ip(50), true}},
		{"season lib: Sonarr S02E01 with absolute 26",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(2), EpisodeNumber: ip(1), AbsoluteNumber: ip(26)}, want{2, 1, ip(26), true}},
		{"season lib: S01E26 past end of season 1 is absolute",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(26)}, want{2, 1, ip(26), true}},
		{"season lib: absolute-only file in Season 3 folder",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(3), EpisodeNumber: ip(50), AbsoluteNumber: ip(50)}, want{3, 13, ip(50), true}},
		{"season lib: no season, absolute only",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, AbsoluteNumber: ip(60)}, want{4, 1, ip(60), true}},
		{"season lib: counted absolutes when provider gave none",
			aotCounted, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(4), EpisodeNumber: ip(2)}, want{4, 2, ip(61), true}},
		{"season lib: unknown absolute stays nil, not guessed",
			aotPartial, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(3), EpisodeNumber: ip(13)}, want{3, 13, nil, true}},
		{"season lib: episode not in metadata yet is passed through",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(5), EpisodeNumber: ip(1)}, want{5, 1, nil, true}},
		{"special passes through",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(0), EpisodeNumber: ip(8)}, want{0, 8, nil, true}},
		{"special without episode number",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(0)}, want{0, 0, nil, false}},
		{"absolute lib: S01E060 is absolute 60",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libAbsolute, SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(60), AbsoluteNumber: ip(60)}, want{4, 1, ip(60), true}},
		{"absolute lib: episode only",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libAbsolute, EpisodeNumber: ip(38)}, want{3, 1, ip(38), true}},
		{"absolute lib: AbsoluteNumber wins over a season folder",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libAbsolute, SeasonNumber: ip(3), EpisodeNumber: ip(1), AbsoluteNumber: ip(50)}, want{3, 13, ip(50), true}},
		{"absolute lib: beyond metadata and no ranges",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libAbsolute, SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(95)}, want{0, 0, nil, false}},
		{"absolute lib: One Piece from anime-lists ranges",
			op, media.Item{LibraryID: libAbsolute, SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(100)}, want{7, 9, ip(100), true}},
		{"absolute lib: One Piece open-ended range",
			op, media.Item{LibraryID: libAbsolute, EpisodeNumber: ip(1200)}, want{23, 45, ip(1200), true}},
		{"season lib: One Piece S01E1094 is absolute",
			op, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(1094), AbsoluteNumber: ip(1094)}, want{22, 9, ip(1094), true}},
		{"season lib: One Piece S07E09 gets absolute from ranges",
			op, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(7), EpisodeNumber: ip(9)}, want{7, 9, ip(100), true}},
		{"library lookup error falls back to season numbering",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libBroken, SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(60)}, want{4, 1, ip(60), true}},
		{"no ids, no seasons, SxxEyy passes through",
			noIDs, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, SeasonNumber: ip(2), EpisodeNumber: ip(3)}, want{2, 3, nil, true}},
		{"no ids, absolute only cannot be placed",
			noIDs, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason, AbsoluteNumber: ip(7)}, want{0, 0, nil, false}},
		{"nothing to map",
			aot, media.Item{LibraryID: libSeason}, want{0, 0, nil, false}},
	}
	m := newTestMapper()
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s, e, a, ok := m.Map(context.Background(), c.show, c.item)
			if ok != c.want.ok || s != c.want.season || e != c.want.episode {
				t.Fatalf("got (%d,%d,%v), want (%d,%d,%v)", s, e, ok, c.want.season, c.want.episode, c.want.ok)
			}
			switch {
			case c.want.abs == nil && a != nil:
				t.Fatalf("absolute = %d, want nil", *a)
			case c.want.abs != nil && (a == nil || *a != *c.want.abs):
				t.Fatalf("absolute = %v, want %d", a, *c.want.abs)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestMapperIsEpisodeMapper(t *testing.T) {
	var _ metadata.EpisodeMapper = newTestMapper()
	// A nil Lookup and nil Libraries must not panic.
	m := NewMapper(nil, nil, nil)
	if s, e, _, ok := m.Map(context.Background(), metadata.Record{}, media.Item{SeasonNumber: ip(1), EpisodeNumber: ip(2)}); !ok || s != 1 || e != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("nil deps: (%d,%d,%v)", s, e, ok)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap/mapper.go`:
```go
package epmap

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sort"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/media"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
)

// Lookup returns the anime-lists entries of a TVDB series (animelists.Store).
type Lookup interface {
	EntriesByTVDB(ctx context.Context, tvdbID int) (Series, error)
}

// Libraries reads a library's options (library.Store).
type Libraries interface {
	Get(ctx context.Context, id int64) (library.Library, error)
}

// Mapper implements metadata.EpisodeMapper for anime libraries.
type Mapper struct {
	lookup Lookup
	libs   Libraries
	log    *slog.Logger
}

var _ metadata.EpisodeMapper = (*Mapper)(nil)

// NewMapper builds a Mapper. Any argument may be nil.
func NewMapper(l Lookup, libs Libraries, log *slog.Logger) *Mapper {
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	}
	return &Mapper{lookup: l, libs: libs, log: log}
}

// Map places ep in show's TVDB-layout seasons. See plan 1E Task 7 for the rules.
func (m *Mapper) Map(ctx context.Context, show metadata.Record, ep media.Item) (int, int, *int, bool) {
	absMode := m.absoluteMode(ctx, ep.LibraryID)
	lay := newLayout(show.Seasons)
	series := m.series(ctx, show)

	if ep.SeasonNumber != nil && *ep.SeasonNumber == 0 {
		if ep.EpisodeNumber == nil {
			return 0, 0, nil, false
		}
		return 0, *ep.EpisodeNumber, nil, true
	}
	abs := 0
	if ep.AbsoluteNumber != nil {
		abs = *ep.AbsoluteNumber
	}
	if absMode && abs == 0 && ep.EpisodeNumber != nil && (ep.SeasonNumber == nil || *ep.SeasonNumber == 1) {
		abs = *ep.EpisodeNumber
	}
	if abs > 0 && (absMode || ep.SeasonNumber == nil || ep.EpisodeNumber == nil) {
		return fromAbsolute(lay, series, abs)
	}
	if ep.SeasonNumber == nil || ep.EpisodeNumber == nil {
		return 0, 0, nil, false
	}
	s, e := *ep.SeasonNumber, *ep.EpisodeNumber
	if a, ok := lay.absolute(s, e); ok {
		return s, e, &a, true
	}
	if !lay.has(s, e) {
		if s == 1 && lay.counts[1] > 0 && e > lay.counts[1] {
			if rs, re, ra, ok := fromAbsolute(lay, series, e); ok {
				return rs, re, ra, true
			}
		}
		if abs > 0 {
			if rs, re, ra, ok := fromAbsolute(lay, series, abs); ok {
				return rs, re, ra, true
			}
		}
		// S01E1094-style names when the record has no episodes yet
		if s == 1 && len(lay.counts) == 0 {
			if rs, re, ok := series.AbsoluteToTVDB(e); ok {
				return rs, re, intp(e), true
			}
		}
	}
	if a, ok := series.TVDBToAbsolute(s, e); ok {
		return s, e, &a, true
	}
	return s, e, nil, true
}

func (m *Mapper) absoluteMode(ctx context.Context, libraryID int64) bool {
	if m.libs == nil {
		return false
	}
	lib, err := m.libs.Get(ctx, libraryID)
	if err != nil {
		m.log.Warn("epmap: library lookup failed, using season numbering", "library", libraryID, "err", err)
		return false
	}
	return lib.Options.AnimeNumbering == "absolute"
}

func (m *Mapper) series(ctx context.Context, show metadata.Record) Series {
	tvdb, ok := anime.IDInt(show.IDs, "tvdb")
	if !ok || m.lookup == nil {
		return nil
	}
	s, err := m.lookup.EntriesByTVDB(ctx, tvdb)
	if err != nil {
		m.log.Warn("epmap: anime-lists lookup failed", "tvdb", tvdb, "err", err)
		return nil
	}
	return s
}

func fromAbsolute(lay layout, series Series, abs int) (int, int, *int, bool) {
	if s, e, ok := lay.fromAbsolute(abs); ok {
		return s, e, intp(abs), true
	}
	if s, e, ok := series.AbsoluteToTVDB(abs); ok {
		return s, e, intp(abs), true
	}
	return 0, 0, nil, false
}

func intp(n int) *int { return &n }

// layout indexes a record's seasons: which (season, episode) pairs exist and
// their absolute numbers.
type layout struct {
	abs    map[[2]int]int // 0 = absolute unknown
	byAbs  map[int][2]int
	counts map[int]int
}

func newLayout(seasons []metadata.SeasonRecord) layout {
	l := layout{abs: map[[2]int]int{}, byAbs: map[int][2]int{}, counts: map[int]int{}}
	type ref struct {
		s, e int
		abs  *int
	}
	var regular []ref
	provided := false
	for _, s := range seasons {
		for _, e := range s.Episodes {
			k := [2]int{s.Number, e.Number}
			if _, dup := l.abs[k]; dup {
				continue
			}
			l.abs[k] = 0
			l.counts[s.Number]++
			if s.Number >= 1 {
				regular = append(regular, ref{s.Number, e.Number, e.Absolute})
				if e.Absolute != nil {
					provided = true
				}
			}
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(regular, func(i, j int) bool {
		if regular[i].s != regular[j].s {
			return regular[i].s < regular[j].s
		}
		return regular[i].e < regular[j].e
	})
	for i, r := range regular {
		a := i + 1
		if provided {
			if r.abs == nil || *r.abs <= 0 {
				continue
			}
			a = *r.abs
		}
		k := [2]int{r.s, r.e}
		l.abs[k] = a
		if _, dup := l.byAbs[a]; !dup {
			l.byAbs[a] = k
		}
	}
	return l
}

func (l layout) has(s, e int) bool { _, ok := l.abs[[2]int{s, e}]; return ok }

func (l layout) absolute(s, e int) (int, bool) {
	a := l.abs[[2]int{s, e}]
	return a, a > 0
}

func (l layout) fromAbsolute(a int) (int, int, bool) {
	k, ok := l.byAbs[a]
	return k[0], k[1], ok
}
```

---

### Task 8: AniList provider

AniList is the primary source of descriptive fields and art for anime (spec 3.2). One GraphQL
query fetches everything for a media (cached 24 h, 7 days once `status` is `FINISHED`/`CANCELLED`);
searches are cached 24 h. Requests are spaced 2 s apart by default (degraded 30 req/min) and 429s
are handled by `httpc`.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist/anilist.go`, `anilist/record.go`
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist/testdata/media_104578.json`, `testdata/search_aot3.json`, `testdata/not_found.json`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist/anilist_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `metadata.Provider`, `metadata.Query`, `metadata.Candidate`, `metadata.Record`, `metadata.Image`,
  `anime.*` helpers, `animecache.Cache`, `httpc.Client`, `httpctest.Clock`.
- Produces:
  - `anilist.Name = "anilist"`, `anilist.DefaultEndpoint`, `anilist.DefaultInterval = 2 * time.Second`
  - `anilist.New(endpoint string, hc *httpc.Client, cache *animecache.Cache) *Provider` ("" endpoint = default)
  - `(*Provider)` implements `metadata.Provider` (`Supports` = anime show/movie); `Fetch` needs
    `ids["anilist"]` or `ids["mal"]`; `Search` uses known ids first, then title search
    (formats `TV, TV_SHORT, ONA, OVA, SPECIAL` for shows, `MOVIE` for movies) scored with `anime.MatchScore`
    over English, romaji, native and synonyms.
  - `(*Provider).Relations(ctx, anilistID int) ([]Relation, error)`; `type Relation struct{ Type string; AniListID int; Format string }` (ANIME nodes only; types like `PREQUEL`, `SEQUEL`, `SIDE_STORY`)
  - `(*Provider).Status() anime.Status` (always enabled)
  - Record: `Title` English (else romaji), `OriginalTitle` native, `AltTitles` en/x-jat/ja, `Overview`
    via `anime.StripHTML`, `Year`/`Released` from `startDate`, `Genres`, `Studios` (main studios),
    `Tags` (rank ≥ 60, no spoilers), `Ratings["anilist"] = averageScore/10` (meanScore fallback),
    `Images` poster (`coverImage.extraLarge`) and banner (`bannerImage`), `IDs` anilist + mal.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist/anilist_test.go`:
```go
package anilist

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"os"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/media"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc/httpctest"
)

var _ metadata.Provider = (*Provider)(nil)

type fakeAniList struct {
	t         *testing.T
	hits      atomic.Int32
	limitOnce atomic.Bool // answer the next request with 429
}

func (f *fakeAniList) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	f.hits.Add(1)
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost || r.Header.Get("Content-Type") != "application/json" {
		f.t.Errorf("bad request %s %s", r.Method, r.Header.Get("Content-Type"))
	}
	var req struct {
		Query     string         `json:"query"`
		Variables map[string]any `json:"variables"`
	}
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		f.t.Errorf("decode: %v", err)
		return
	}
	if f.limitOnce.CompareAndSwap(true, false) {
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", "2")
		w.Header().Set("X-RateLimit-Remaining", "0")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusTooManyRequests)
		io.WriteString(w, `{"data":null,"errors":[{"message":"Too Many Requests.","status":429}]}`)
		return
	}
	file := "testdata/not_found.json"
	switch {
	case strings.Contains(req.Query, "Page("):
		if req.Variables["search"] == nil || req.Variables["formats"] == nil {
			f.t.Errorf("search variables missing: %v", req.Variables)
		}
		file = "testdata/search_aot3.json"
	case req.Variables["id"] == float64(104578) || req.Variables["idMal"] == float64(38524):
		file = "testdata/media_104578.json"
	default:
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
	}
	b, err := os.ReadFile(file)
	if err != nil {
		f.t.Errorf("fixture: %v", err)
		return
	}
	w.Write(b)
}

func newProvider(t *testing.T) (*Provider, *fakeAniList, *httpctest.Clock) {
	t.Helper()
	fake := &fakeAniList{t: t}
	srv := httptest.NewServer(fake)
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	cache := animecache.New(dbtest.Open(t))
	cache.Now = clock.Now
	return New(srv.URL, clock.Client(DefaultInterval), cache), fake, clock
}

func TestSupports(t *testing.T) {
	p, _, _ := newProvider(t)
	if !p.Supports(anime.KindShow, anime.LibAnime) || !p.Supports(anime.KindMovie, anime.LibAnime) ||
		p.Supports(anime.KindShow, library.Type("tv")) || p.Supports(media.Kind("episode"), anime.LibAnime) {
		t.Fatal("Supports must be anime show/movie only")
	}
	if p.Name() != "anilist" || !p.Status().Enabled {
		t.Fatal("name/status")
	}
}

func TestFetchRecord(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, _ := newProvider(t)
	rec, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anilist": "104578"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := metadata.Record{
		Provider:      "anilist",
		IDs:           map[string]string{"anilist": "104578", "mal": "38524"},
		Title:         "Attack on Titan Season 3 Part 2",
		OriginalTitle: "進撃の巨人 Season３ Part.2",
		AltTitles: map[string]string{"en": "Attack on Titan Season 3 Part 2",
			"x-jat": "Shingeki no Kyojin Season 3 Part 2", "ja": "進撃の巨人 Season３ Part.2"},
		Overview: "The battle to retake Wall Maria begins now! With Eren’s new hardening ability, the Scouts are confident they can seal the wall and take back Shiganshina District.",
		Year:     2019,
		Released: "2019-04-29",
		Genres:   []string{"Action", "Drama", "Fantasy", "Mystery"},
		Studios:  []string{"WIT STUDIO"},
		Tags:     []string{"Military", "Survival", "Gore"},
		Ratings:  map[string]float64{"anilist": 8.8},
		Images: []metadata.Image{
			{Type: "poster", URL: "https://s4.anilist.co/file/anilistcdn/media/anime/cover/large/bx104578-k61nx3LPjvgd.jpg"},
			{Type: "banner", URL: "https://s4.anilist.co/file/anilistcdn/media/anime/banner/104578-z7SadpYEuAsy.jpg"},
		},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(rec, want) {
		t.Fatalf("record:\n got %+v\nwant %+v", rec, want)
	}
	// Second fetch comes from the cache.
	if _, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anilist": "104578"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("hits = %d, want 1", fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestFetchByMALAndNotFound(t *testing.T) {
	p, _, _ := newProvider(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	rec, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"mal": "38524"})
	if err != nil || rec.IDs["anilist"] != "104578" {
		t.Fatalf("by mal: %v %v", rec.IDs, err)
	}
	if _, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anilist": "1"}); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("missing media: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"tvdb": "267440"}); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNoUsableID) {
		t.Fatalf("no usable id: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestFetchCacheExpiresAfterTTL(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, clock := newProvider(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	ids := map[string]string{"anilist": "104578"}
	p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, ids)
	clock.Advance(3 * 24 * time.Hour) // FINISHED → 7 day TTL
	p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, ids)
	if fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("finished show refetched too early: %d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
	clock.Advance(5 * 24 * time.Hour)
	p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, ids)
	if fake.hits.Load() != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expired entry not refetched: %d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestRateLimitedThenSucceeds(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, clock := newProvider(t)
	fake.limitOnce.Store(true)
	if _, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anilist": "104578"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("hits = %d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
	slept := clock.Slept()
	if len(slept) == 0 || slept[len(slept)-1] < 2*time.Second {
		t.Fatalf("Retry-After not honoured: %v", slept)
	}
}

func TestSearchScoresAllTitles(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, _ := newProvider(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	q := metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindShow, LibType: anime.LibAnime, Title: "Attack on Titan Season 3", Year: 2018}
	got, err := p.Search(ctx, q)
	if err != nil || len(got) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("got %d candidates, err %v", len(got), err)
	}
	if got[0].IDs["anilist"] != "99147" || got[0].Score != 1 || got[0].Year != 2018 || got[0].Provider != "anilist" {
		t.Fatalf("best = %+v", got[0])
	}
	if got[1].IDs["anilist"] != "104578" || got[1].Score >= 1 {
		t.Fatalf("second = %+v", got[1])
	}
	if _, ok := got[2].IDs["mal"]; ok {
		t.Fatal("null idMal must not produce a mal id")
	}
	// Native-title search scores against the native title too (same fixture, served again from cache key per title).
	nat, _ := p.Search(ctx, metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindShow, LibType: anime.LibAnime, Title: "進撃の巨人 Season３"})
	if len(nat) == 0 || nat[0].IDs["anilist"] != "99147" || nat[0].Score != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("native search best = %+v", nat)
	}
	// Repeating a search hits the cache.
	before := fake.hits.Load()
	p.Search(ctx, q)
	if fake.hits.Load() != before {
		t.Fatal("search not cached")
	}
}

func TestSearchWithKnownIDSkipsTitleSearch(t *testing.T) {
	p, _, _ := newProvider(t)
	got, err := p.Search(context.Background(), metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindShow, LibType: anime.LibAnime,
		Title: "whatever", IDs: map[string]string{"mal": "38524"}})
	if err != nil || len(got) != 1 || got[0].IDs["anilist"] != "104578" || got[0].Score != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v err %v", got, err)
	}
}

func TestRelations(t *testing.T) {
	p, _, _ := newProvider(t)
	got, err := p.Relations(context.Background(), 104578)
	want := []Relation{{Type: "PREQUEL", AniListID: 99147, Format: "TV"}, {Type: "SEQUEL", AniListID: 110277, Format: "TV"}}
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("relations %+v err %v", got, err)
	}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist/anilist.go`:
```go
// Package anilist is the AniList GraphQL metadata provider for anime libraries.
//
// API: POST https://graphql.anilist.co, no key for public queries. Rate limit
// 90 req/min, degraded to 30 req/min since 2025 (see docs.anilist.co/guide/rate-limiting);
// 429 responses carry Retry-After and X-RateLimit-Reset.
package anilist

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/media"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc"
)

const (
	Name            = "anilist"
	DefaultEndpoint = "https://graphql.anilist.co"
	DefaultInterval = 2 * time.Second // 30 requests per minute
)

const mediaFields = `id idMal type format status episodes isAdult
title { romaji english native }
synonyms
description(asHtml: false)
startDate { year month day }
endDate { year month day }
genres
tags { name rank isMediaSpoiler isGeneralSpoiler }
studios(isMain: true) { nodes { name } }
averageScore meanScore
coverImage { extraLarge large }
bannerImage
relations { edges { relationType node { id type format } } }`

const fetchQuery = `query ($id: Int, $idMal: Int) { Media(id: $id, idMal: $idMal, type: ANIME) { ` + mediaFields + ` } }`

const searchQuery = `query ($search: String, $formats: [MediaFormat]) { Page(page: 1, perPage: 10) {
media(search: $search, type: ANIME, format_in: $formats, sort: SEARCH_MATCH) {
id idMal format title { romaji english native } synonyms startDate { year } } } }`

var (
	showFormats  = []string{"TV", "TV_SHORT", "ONA", "OVA", "SPECIAL"}
	movieFormats = []string{"MOVIE"}
)

// FuzzyDate is AniList's partial date.
type FuzzyDate struct {
	Year  int `json:"year"`
	Month int `json:"month"`
	Day   int `json:"day"`
}

// String is YYYY-MM-DD, or "" when any part is missing.
func (d FuzzyDate) String() string {
	if d.Year == 0 || d.Month == 0 || d.Day == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%04d-%02d-%02d", d.Year, d.Month, d.Day)
}

// Tag is a media tag.
type Tag struct {
	Name             string `json:"name"`
	Rank             int    `json:"rank"`
	IsMediaSpoiler   bool   `json:"isMediaSpoiler"`
	IsGeneralSpoiler bool   `json:"isGeneralSpoiler"`
}

// Media is the subset of AniList's Media type that 1E reads.
type Media struct {
	ID       int    `json:"id"`
	IDMal    int    `json:"idMal"`
	Type     string `json:"type"`
	Format   string `json:"format"`
	Status   string `json:"status"`
	Episodes int    `json:"episodes"`
	IsAdult  bool   `json:"isAdult"`
	Title    struct {
		Romaji  string `json:"romaji"`
		English string `json:"english"`
		Native  string `json:"native"`
	} `json:"title"`
	Synonyms    []string  `json:"synonyms"`
	Description string    `json:"description"`
	StartDate   FuzzyDate `json:"startDate"`
	EndDate     FuzzyDate `json:"endDate"`
	Genres      []string  `json:"genres"`
	Tags        []Tag     `json:"tags"`
	Studios     struct {
		Nodes []struct {
			Name string `json:"name"`
		} `json:"nodes"`
	} `json:"studios"`
	AverageScore int `json:"averageScore"`
	MeanScore    int `json:"meanScore"`
	CoverImage   struct {
		ExtraLarge string `json:"extraLarge"`
		Large      string `json:"large"`
	} `json:"coverImage"`
	BannerImage string `json:"bannerImage"`
	Relations   struct {
		Edges []struct {
			RelationType string `json:"relationType"`
			Node         struct {
				ID     int    `json:"id"`
				Type   string `json:"type"`
				Format string `json:"format"`
			} `json:"node"`
		} `json:"edges"`
	} `json:"relations"`
}

// Relation is a related anime (prequel, sequel, side story, ...).
type Relation struct {
	Type      string
	AniListID int
	Format    string
}

// Provider implements metadata.Provider for AniList.
type Provider struct {
	endpoint string
	http     *httpc.Client
	cache    *animecache.Cache
}

var _ metadata.Provider = (*Provider)(nil)

// New returns the provider. hc should use a limiter of DefaultInterval (or 60s/RPM).
func New(endpoint string, hc *httpc.Client, cache *animecache.Cache) *Provider {
	if endpoint == "" {
		endpoint = DefaultEndpoint
	}
	return &Provider{endpoint: endpoint, http: hc, cache: cache}
}

func (p *Provider) Name() string                                    { return Name }
func (p *Provider) Supports(kind media.Kind, lt library.Type) bool { return anime.Supports(kind, lt) }
func (p *Provider) Status() anime.Status                            { return anime.Status{Name: Name, Enabled: true} }

// Fetch returns the record for ids["anilist"] (or ids["mal"]).
func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, kind media.Kind, ids map[string]string) (metadata.Record, error) {
	m, err := p.lookup(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return metadata.Record{}, err
	}
	return toRecord(m), nil
}

// Search uses a known AniList/MAL id first, else a title search.
func (p *Provider) Search(ctx context.Context, q metadata.Query) ([]metadata.Candidate, error) {
	_, hasAL := anime.IDInt(q.IDs, "anilist")
	_, hasMAL := anime.IDInt(q.IDs, "mal")
	if hasAL || hasMAL {
		m, err := p.lookup(ctx, q.IDs)
		if err == nil {
			return []metadata.Candidate{candidate(m, 1)}, nil
		}
		if !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNotFound) {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(q.Title) == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	formats := showFormats
	if q.Kind == anime.KindMovie {
		formats = movieFormats
	}
	key := "search:" + string(q.Kind) + ":" + anime.Normalize(q.Title)
	raw, cached, err := p.data(ctx, key, searchQuery, map[string]any{"search": q.Title, "formats": formats})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var page struct {
		Page struct {
			Media []Media `json:"media"`
		} `json:"Page"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &page); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anilist: decode search: %w", err)
	}
	if !cached {
		if err := p.cache.Put(ctx, Name, key, raw, 24*time.Hour); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	out := make([]metadata.Candidate, 0, len(page.Page.Media))
	for _, m := range page.Page.Media {
		titles := append([]string{m.Title.English, m.Title.Romaji, m.Title.Native}, m.Synonyms...)
		out = append(out, candidate(m, anime.MatchScore(q.Title, q.Year, titles, m.StartDate.Year)))
	}
	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Score > out[j].Score })
	return out, nil
}

// Relations lists related anime of an AniList media (manga etc. are skipped).
func (p *Provider) Relations(ctx context.Context, anilistID int) ([]Relation, error) {
	m, err := p.lookup(ctx, map[string]string{"anilist": strconv.Itoa(anilistID)})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []Relation
	for _, e := range m.Relations.Edges {
		if e.Node.Type != "ANIME" {
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, Relation{Type: e.RelationType, AniListID: e.Node.ID, Format: e.Node.Format})
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (p *Provider) lookup(ctx context.Context, ids map[string]string) (Media, error) {
	if id, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "anilist"); ok {
		return p.media(ctx, "media:"+strconv.Itoa(id), map[string]any{"id": id})
	}
	if mal, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "mal"); ok {
		return p.media(ctx, "mal:"+strconv.Itoa(mal), map[string]any{"idMal": mal})
	}
	return Media{}, fmt.Errorf("anilist: no anilist or mal id: %w", anime.ErrNoUsableID)
}

func (p *Provider) media(ctx context.Context, key string, vars map[string]any) (Media, error) {
	raw, cached, err := p.data(ctx, key, fetchQuery, vars)
	if err != nil {
		return Media{}, err
	}
	var d struct {
		Media *Media `json:"Media"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &d); err != nil {
		return Media{}, fmt.Errorf("anilist: decode media: %w", err)
	}
	if d.Media == nil {
		return Media{}, fmt.Errorf("anilist %s: %w", key, anime.ErrNotFound)
	}
	if !cached {
		ttl := 24 * time.Hour
		if d.Media.Status == "FINISHED" || d.Media.Status == "CANCELLED" {
			ttl = 7 * 24 * time.Hour
		}
		if err := p.cache.Put(ctx, Name, key, raw, ttl); err != nil {
			return Media{}, err
		}
	}
	return *d.Media, nil
}

// data returns the GraphQL "data" member, from the cache when fresh.
func (p *Provider) data(ctx context.Context, key, query string, vars map[string]any) ([]byte, bool, error) {
	if b, ok, err := p.cache.Fresh(ctx, Name, key); err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	} else if ok {
		return b, true, nil
	}
	payload, err := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"query": query, "variables": vars})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}
	resp, err := p.http.Do(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) (*http.Request, error) {
		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, p.endpoint, bytes.NewReader(payload))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
		return req, nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("anilist: %w", err)
	}
	var env struct {
		Data   json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
		Errors []struct {
			Message string `json:"message"`
			Status  int    `json:"status"`
		} `json:"errors"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(resp.Body, &env); err != nil {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("anilist: status %d: decode: %w", resp.Status, err)
	}
	if resp.Status == http.StatusNotFound || (len(env.Errors) > 0 && env.Errors[0].Status == http.StatusNotFound) {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("anilist %s: %w", key, anime.ErrNotFound)
	}
	if len(env.Errors) > 0 {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("anilist: %s (status %d)", env.Errors[0].Message, env.Errors[0].Status)
	}
	if resp.Status != http.StatusOK || len(env.Data) == 0 || string(env.Data) == "null" {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("anilist: unexpected status %d", resp.Status)
	}
	return env.Data, false, nil
}

func candidate(m Media, score float64) metadata.Candidate {
	return metadata.Candidate{Provider: Name, IDs: idsOf(m), Title: preferredTitle(m), Year: m.StartDate.Year, Score: score}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist/record.go`:
```go
package anilist

import (
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
)

func idsOf(m Media) map[string]string {
	ids := map[string]string{}
	anime.SetID(ids, "anilist", m.ID)
	anime.SetID(ids, "mal", m.IDMal)
	return ids
}

func preferredTitle(m Media) string {
	if m.Title.English != "" {
		return m.Title.English
	}
	return m.Title.Romaji
}

func toRecord(m Media) metadata.Record {
	rec := metadata.Record{
		Provider:      Name,
		IDs:           idsOf(m),
		Title:         preferredTitle(m),
		OriginalTitle: m.Title.Native,
		AltTitles:     anime.AltTitles(m.Title.English, m.Title.Romaji, m.Title.Native),
		Overview:      anime.StripHTML(m.Description),
		Year:          m.StartDate.Year,
		Released:      m.StartDate.String(),
		Genres:        m.Genres,
		Ratings:       map[string]float64{},
	}
	for _, s := range m.Studios.Nodes {
		if s.Name != "" {
			rec.Studios = append(rec.Studios, s.Name)
		}
	}
	for _, t := range m.Tags {
		if t.Rank >= 60 && !t.IsMediaSpoiler && !t.IsGeneralSpoiler {
			rec.Tags = append(rec.Tags, t.Name)
		}
	}
	score := m.AverageScore
	if score == 0 {
		score = m.MeanScore
	}
	if score > 0 {
		rec.Ratings["anilist"] = float64(score) / 10
	}
	cover := m.CoverImage.ExtraLarge
	if cover == "" {
		cover = m.CoverImage.Large
	}
	if cover != "" {
		rec.Images = append(rec.Images, metadata.Image{Type: "poster", URL: cover})
	}
	if m.BannerImage != "" {
		rec.Images = append(rec.Images, metadata.Image{Type: "banner", URL: m.BannerImage})
	}
	return rec
}
```

### Task 9: Jikan (MyAnimeList) provider

Jikan supplies the MAL score, MAL's content rating (AniList and AniDB have none usable for
profile limits) and fallback titles/art. One request per second (Jikan allows 3/s and 60/min).
Responses are cached 24 h (7 days for `Finished Airing`).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/jikan/jikan.go`
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/jikan/testdata/anime_16498.json`, `testdata/search_aot.json`, `testdata/not_found.json`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/jikan/jikan_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `metadata.Provider` types, `anime.*`, `animecache.Cache`, `httpc.Client`, `httpctest.Clock`.
- Produces:
  - `jikan.Name = "jikan"`, `jikan.DefaultBaseURL = "https://api.jikan.moe/v4"`, `jikan.DefaultInterval = time.Second`
  - `jikan.New(base string, hc *httpc.Client, cache *animecache.Cache) *Provider` ("" base = default)
  - `(*Provider)` implements `metadata.Provider` (anime show/movie); `Fetch` needs `ids["mal"]`;
    `Search` uses `ids["mal"]` first, else `GET /anime?q=…&limit=10` (`&type=movie` for movies;
    for shows, results of type `Movie`/`Music` are dropped).
  - `(*Provider).Status() anime.Status`
  - `jikan.ContentRating(malRating string) string`: `"G - All Ages"`→`G`, `"PG - Children"`→`PG`,
    `"PG-13 - Teens 13 or older"`→`PG-13`, `"R - 17+ (violence & profanity)"`→`R`,
    `"R+ - Mild Nudity"`→`R+`, `"Rx - Hentai"`→`X`, else `""`.
  - Record: `Ratings["mal"] = score`, `ContentRating`, `Title` English (else default title),
    `OriginalTitle` Japanese, `AltTitles`, `Overview` (`anime.StripHTML` of synopsis), `Year`,
    `Released`, `Genres`, `Studios`, poster from `images.jpg.large_image_url`, `IDs["mal"]`.
