`server/internal/metadata/anime/jikan/jikan_test.go`:
```go
package jikan

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"os"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc/httpctest"
)

var _ metadata.Provider = (*Provider)(nil)

type fakeJikan struct {
	t         *testing.T
	hits      atomic.Int32
	lastQuery atomic.Value
	limitOnce atomic.Bool
}

func (f *fakeJikan) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	f.hits.Add(1)
	f.lastQuery.Store(r.URL.RawQuery)
	if f.limitOnce.CompareAndSwap(true, false) {
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", "1")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusTooManyRequests)
		w.Write([]byte(`{"status":429,"type":"RateLimitException","message":"You are being rate-limited."}`))
		return
	}
	file := "testdata/not_found.json"
	switch r.URL.Path {
	case "/anime/16498":
		file = "testdata/anime_16498.json"
	case "/anime":
		file = "testdata/search_aot.json"
	default:
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
	}
	b, err := os.ReadFile(file)
	if err != nil {
		f.t.Errorf("fixture: %v", err)
		return
	}
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.Write(b)
}

func newProvider(t *testing.T) (*Provider, *fakeJikan, *httpctest.Clock) {
	t.Helper()
	fake := &fakeJikan{t: t}
	srv := httptest.NewServer(fake)
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	cache := animecache.New(dbtest.Open(t))
	cache.Now = clock.Now
	return New(srv.URL, clock.Client(DefaultInterval), cache), fake, clock
}

func TestFetchRecord(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, _ := newProvider(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	rec, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"mal": "16498"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := metadata.Record{
		Provider:      "jikan",
		IDs:           map[string]string{"mal": "16498"},
		Title:         "Attack on Titan",
		OriginalTitle: "進撃の巨人",
		AltTitles:     map[string]string{"en": "Attack on Titan", "x-jat": "Shingeki no Kyojin", "ja": "進撃の巨人"},
		Overview:      "Centuries ago, mankind was slaughtered to near extinction by monstrous humanoid creatures called Titans.",
		Year:          2013,
		Released:      "2013-04-07",
		Genres:        []string{"Action", "Drama", "Mystery"},
		Studios:       []string{"Wit Studio"},
		ContentRating: "R",
		Ratings:       map[string]float64{"mal": 8.55},
		Images:        []metadata.Image{{Type: "poster", URL: "https://cdn.myanimelist.net/images/anime/10/47347l.jpg"}},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(rec, want) {
		t.Fatalf("record:\n got %+v\nwant %+v", rec, want)
	}
	p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"mal": "16498"})
	if fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("not cached: hits=%d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestFetchErrors(t *testing.T) {
	p, _, _ := newProvider(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"mal": "1"}); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("404: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anidb": "9541"}); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNoUsableID) {
		t.Fatalf("no mal id: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRateLimitRetried(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, clock := newProvider(t)
	fake.limitOnce.Store(true)
	if _, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"mal": "16498"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 2 || !slices.Contains(clock.Slept(), time.Second) {
		t.Fatalf("hits=%d slept=%v", fake.hits.Load(), clock.Slept())
	}
}

func TestSearch(t *testing.T) {
	p, fake, _ := newProvider(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	got, err := p.Search(ctx, metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindShow, LibType: anime.LibAnime, Title: "Attack on Titan", Year: 2013})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(got) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("movie result must be dropped for a show search: %+v", got)
	}
	if got[0].IDs["mal"] != "16498" || got[0].Score != 1 || got[0].Year != 2013 || got[0].Provider != "jikan" {
		t.Fatalf("best = %+v", got[0])
	}
	if q, _ := fake.lastQuery.Load().(string); q != "limit=10&q=Attack+on+Titan" {
		t.Fatalf("query = %q", q)
	}
	movies, _ := p.Search(ctx, metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindMovie, LibType: anime.LibAnime, Title: "Attack on Titan Crimson Bow and Arrow"})
	if q, _ := fake.lastQuery.Load().(string); q != "limit=10&q=Attack+on+Titan+Crimson+Bow+and+Arrow&type=movie" {
		t.Fatalf("movie query = %q", q)
	}
	if len(movies) == 0 || movies[0].IDs["mal"] != "23775" || movies[0].Year != 2014 {
		t.Fatalf("movie search = %+v", movies)
	}
	byID, err := p.Search(ctx, metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindShow, LibType: anime.LibAnime, IDs: map[string]string{"mal": "16498"}})
	if err != nil || len(byID) != 1 || byID[0].Score != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("by id = %+v %v", byID, err)
	}
}

func TestContentRating(t *testing.T) {
	cases := map[string]string{
		"G - All Ages":                   "G",
		"PG - Children":                  "PG",
		"PG-13 - Teens 13 or older":      "PG-13",
		"R - 17+ (violence & profanity)": "R",
		"R+ - Mild Nudity":               "R+",
		"Rx - Hentai":                    "X",
		"":                               "",
		"None":                           "",
	}
	for in, want := range cases {
		if got := ContentRating(in); got != want {
			t.Errorf("ContentRating(%q) = %q, want %q", in, got, want)
		}
	}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/jikan/jikan.go`:
```go
// Package jikan is the Jikan v4 (unofficial MyAnimeList API) provider: MAL
// score, MAL content rating and fallback data for anime libraries.
//
// Limits (docs.api.jikan.moe): 3 requests/second and 60 requests/minute; 429
// when exceeded. This provider sends at most one request per second.
package jikan

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/media"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc"
)

const (
	Name            = "jikan"
	DefaultBaseURL  = "https://api.jikan.moe/v4"
	DefaultInterval = time.Second
)

type named struct {
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

// Anime is the subset of Jikan's anime object that 1E reads.
type Anime struct {
	MalID         int      `json:"mal_id"`
	Title         string   `json:"title"`
	TitleEnglish  string   `json:"title_english"`
	TitleJapanese string   `json:"title_japanese"`
	TitleSynonyms []string `json:"title_synonyms"`
	Type          string   `json:"type"`
	Episodes      int      `json:"episodes"`
	Status        string   `json:"status"`
	Aired         struct {
		From string `json:"from"`
	} `json:"aired"`
	Rating   string  `json:"rating"`
	Score    float64 `json:"score"`
	Synopsis string  `json:"synopsis"`
	Year     int     `json:"year"`
	Images   struct {
		JPG struct {
			ImageURL      string `json:"image_url"`
			LargeImageURL string `json:"large_image_url"`
		} `json:"jpg"`
	} `json:"images"`
	Genres  []named `json:"genres"`
	Studios []named `json:"studios"`
}

func (a Anime) year() int {
	if a.Year > 0 {
		return a.Year
	}
	if len(a.Aired.From) >= 4 {
		y, _ := strconv.Atoi(a.Aired.From[:4])
		return y
	}
	return 0
}

// Provider implements metadata.Provider for Jikan.
type Provider struct {
	base  string
	http  *httpc.Client
	cache *animecache.Cache
}

var _ metadata.Provider = (*Provider)(nil)

func New(base string, hc *httpc.Client, cache *animecache.Cache) *Provider {
	if base == "" {
		base = DefaultBaseURL
	}
	return &Provider{base: strings.TrimRight(base, "/"), http: hc, cache: cache}
}

func (p *Provider) Name() string                                    { return Name }
func (p *Provider) Supports(kind media.Kind, lt library.Type) bool { return anime.Supports(kind, lt) }
func (p *Provider) Status() anime.Status                            { return anime.Status{Name: Name, Enabled: true} }

// Fetch returns the record for ids["mal"].
func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, kind media.Kind, ids map[string]string) (metadata.Record, error) {
	a, err := p.anime(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return metadata.Record{}, err
	}
	return toRecord(a), nil
}

// Search uses ids["mal"] first, else a title search.
func (p *Provider) Search(ctx context.Context, q metadata.Query) ([]metadata.Candidate, error) {
	if _, ok := anime.IDInt(q.IDs, "mal"); ok {
		a, err := p.anime(ctx, q.IDs)
		if err == nil {
			return []metadata.Candidate{candidate(a, 1)}, nil
		}
		if !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNotFound) {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(q.Title) == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	v := url.Values{"q": {q.Title}, "limit": {"10"}}
	if q.Kind == anime.KindMovie {
		v.Set("type", "movie")
	}
	raw, err := p.get(ctx, "search:"+string(q.Kind)+":"+anime.Normalize(q.Title), "/anime", v, func([]byte) time.Duration { return 24 * time.Hour })
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var list []Anime
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &list); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("jikan: decode search: %w", err)
	}
	var out []metadata.Candidate
	for _, a := range list {
		if q.Kind != anime.KindMovie && (a.Type == "Movie" || a.Type == "Music") {
			continue
		}
		titles := append([]string{a.TitleEnglish, a.Title, a.TitleJapanese}, a.TitleSynonyms...)
		out = append(out, candidate(a, anime.MatchScore(q.Title, q.Year, titles, a.year())))
	}
	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Score > out[j].Score })
	return out, nil
}

func (p *Provider) anime(ctx context.Context, ids map[string]string) (Anime, error) {
	mal, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "mal")
	if !ok {
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("jikan: no mal id: %w", anime.ErrNoUsableID)
	}
	raw, err := p.get(ctx, "anime:"+strconv.Itoa(mal), "/anime/"+strconv.Itoa(mal), nil, func(b []byte) time.Duration {
		var a Anime
		if json.Unmarshal(b, &a) == nil && a.Status == "Finished Airing" {
			return 7 * 24 * time.Hour
		}
		return 24 * time.Hour
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Anime{}, err
	}
	var a Anime
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &a); err != nil {
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("jikan: decode anime %d: %w", mal, err)
	}
	return a, nil
}

// get returns the "data" member of a Jikan response, cached under key.
func (p *Provider) get(ctx context.Context, key, path string, q url.Values, ttl func([]byte) time.Duration) ([]byte, error) {
	if b, ok, err := p.cache.Fresh(ctx, Name, key); err != nil || ok {
		return b, err
	}
	u := p.base + path
	if len(q) > 0 {
		u += "?" + q.Encode()
	}
	resp, err := p.http.Do(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) (*http.Request, error) {
		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, u, nil)
		if err == nil {
			req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
		}
		return req, err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("jikan: %w", err)
	}
	if resp.Status == http.StatusNotFound {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("jikan %s: %w", key, anime.ErrNotFound)
	}
	if resp.Status != http.StatusOK {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("jikan %s: unexpected status %d", key, resp.Status)
	}
	var env struct {
		Data json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(resp.Body, &env); err != nil || len(env.Data) == 0 || string(env.Data) == "null" {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("jikan %s: response without data", key)
	}
	if err := p.cache.Put(ctx, Name, key, env.Data, ttl(env.Data)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return env.Data, nil
}

func candidate(a Anime, score float64) metadata.Candidate {
	ids := map[string]string{}
	anime.SetID(ids, "mal", a.MalID)
	title := a.TitleEnglish
	if title == "" {
		title = a.Title
	}
	return metadata.Candidate{Provider: Name, IDs: ids, Title: title, Year: a.year(), Score: score}
}

// ContentRating converts MAL's rating text to a short rating code.
func ContentRating(malRating string) string {
	code, _, _ := strings.Cut(strings.TrimSpace(malRating), " - ")
	switch code {
	case "G", "PG", "PG-13", "R", "R+":
		return code
	case "Rx":
		return "X"
	}
	return ""
}

func toRecord(a Anime) metadata.Record {
	ids := map[string]string{}
	anime.SetID(ids, "mal", a.MalID)
	title := a.TitleEnglish
	if title == "" {
		title = a.Title
	}
	rec := metadata.Record{
		Provider:      Name,
		IDs:           ids,
		Title:         title,
		OriginalTitle: a.TitleJapanese,
		AltTitles:     anime.AltTitles(a.TitleEnglish, a.Title, a.TitleJapanese),
		Overview:      anime.StripHTML(a.Synopsis),
		Year:          a.year(),
		ContentRating: ContentRating(a.Rating),
		Ratings:       map[string]float64{},
	}
	if len(a.Aired.From) >= 10 {
		rec.Released = a.Aired.From[:10]
	}
	for _, g := range a.Genres {
		rec.Genres = append(rec.Genres, g.Name)
	}
	for _, s := range a.Studios {
		rec.Studios = append(rec.Studios, s.Name)
	}
	if a.Score > 0 {
		rec.Ratings["mal"] = a.Score
	}
	poster := a.Images.JPG.LargeImageURL
	if poster == "" {
		poster = a.Images.JPG.ImageURL
	}
	if poster != "" {
		rec.Images = append(rec.Images, metadata.Image{Type: "poster", URL: poster})
	}
	return rec
}
```

---

### Task 10: AniDB HTTP API client (config, XML, ban-safe fetching)

AniDB is strict, so the client is built to never request an `aid` that is cached and fresh, to
serve stale data rather than call AniDB when it is unhappy, and to stop completely on a ban.

Order inside `Client.Anime(aid)`:
1. Fresh cache entry → parse and return (an `<error>` body cached for a not-found aid → `ErrNotFound`).
2. Disabled (no/invalid client, or AniDB rejected the client) → stale entry if any, else `ErrDisabled`.
3. Banned (persisted in `server_settings` key `anime.anidb.banned_until`, 24 h) or daily cap
   reached (default 1000 requests per UTC day) → stale entry if any, else `ErrRateLimited`.
4. Request through the limiter (2.5 s spacing; config floor 2 s). Network/5xx error → stale if any.
5. `<error>` body: contains "banned" → set ban for 24 h; contains "not found" → cache the error body
   for 24 h, `ErrNotFound`; anything else (e.g. `client version missing or invalid`) → disable with
   that reason, `ErrDisabled`.
6. `<anime>` body → cache for 24 h, or 7 days when `enddate` is more than 30 days in the past.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/config.go`, `anidb/xml.go`, `anidb/client.go`
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/testdata/anime_9541.xml`, `testdata/anime_10944.xml`,
  `testdata/error_banned.xml`, `testdata/error_notfound.xml`, `testdata/error_client.xml`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/config_test.go`, `anidb/client_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `animecache.Cache`, `httpc.Client`, `httpctest.Clock`, `db.DB` (`GetSetting`/`SetSetting`), `anime.*` errors and `Status`.
- Produces:
  - `anidb.Config{Client, ClientVer, BaseURL, TitlesURL string; Interval time.Duration; DailyCap int}`;
    `(Config).WithDefaults() Config`; `(Config).Validate() error` (message is the admin-visible reason)
  - `anidb.DefaultBaseURL = "http://api.anidb.net:9001/httpapi"`, `anidb.DefaultTitlesURL = "https://anidb.net/api/anime-titles.xml.gz"`,
    `anidb.MinInterval = 2 * time.Second`, `anidb.DefaultInterval = 2500 * time.Millisecond`, `anidb.DefaultDailyCap = 1000`,
    `anidb.SettingBannedUntil = "anime.anidb.banned_until"`
  - XML types `anidb.Anime`, `Title`, `Related`, `Creator`, `Ratings`, `Rating`, `Resource`, `Tag`,
    `Episode`, `EpNo`; `(Episode).Key() (anidbSeason, n int, ok bool)` (regular → (1,n), special `S` → (0,n),
    trailer `T` → (0,200+n), other `O` → (0,400+n), credits/parody → not ok); `(Episode).BestTitle() string`
    (en, x-jat, ja, first); `(Rating).Float() float64`
  - `anidb.NewClient(ctx, cfg Config, hc *httpc.Client, cache *animecache.Cache, d *db.DB, now func() time.Time) *Client`
    (cfg is passed through `WithDefaults`; hc's limiter must use `cfg.Interval`)
  - `(*Client).Anime(ctx, aid int) (Anime, error)`, `(*Client).Enabled() bool`, `(*Client).Status() anime.Status`

```go
package anidb

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestConfigValidate(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name    string
		cfg     Config
		wantErr string // "" = valid
	}{
		{"valid", Config{Client: "lisimflix", ClientVer: "1"}, ""},
		{"missing", Config{}, "LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT"},
		{"missing version", Config{Client: "lisimflix"}, "LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER"},
		{"upper case name", Config{Client: "Lisimflix", ClientVer: "1"}, "lowercase"},
		{"version not a number", Config{Client: "lisimflix", ClientVer: "1.0"}, "whole number"},
		{"version zero", Config{Client: "lisimflix", ClientVer: "0"}, "whole number"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		err := c.cfg.Validate()
		switch {
		case c.wantErr == "" && err != nil:
			t.Errorf("%s: unexpected %v", c.name, err)
		case c.wantErr != "" && (err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), c.wantErr)):
			t.Errorf("%s: err %v should mention %q", c.name, err, c.wantErr)
		}
	}
}

func TestConfigDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	c := Config{Client: "x1", ClientVer: "1", Interval: time.Second}.WithDefaults()
	if c.BaseURL != DefaultBaseURL || c.TitlesURL != DefaultTitlesURL || c.DailyCap != DefaultDailyCap {
		t.Fatalf("defaults: %+v", c)
	}
	if c.Interval != MinInterval {
		t.Fatalf("interval below the AniDB floor must be raised: %v", c.Interval)
	}
	if (Config{}).WithDefaults().Interval != DefaultInterval {
		t.Fatal("default interval")
	}
}
```

```go
package anidb

import (
	"compress/gzip"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc/httpctest"
)

// fakeAniDB serves testdata/anime_<aid>.xml (gzip, like AniDB) or an override.
type fakeAniDB struct {
	t      *testing.T
	hits   atomic.Int32
	mu     sync.Mutex
	files  map[string]string // aid → testdata file (default anime_<aid>.xml, else not found)
	status map[string]int    // aid → HTTP status
}

func newFake(t *testing.T) *fakeAniDB {
	return &fakeAniDB{t: t, files: map[string]string{}, status: map[string]int{}}
}

func (f *fakeAniDB) set(aid, file string) { f.mu.Lock(); f.files[aid] = file; f.mu.Unlock() }

func (f *fakeAniDB) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	f.hits.Add(1)
	q := r.URL.Query()
	if q.Get("request") != "anime" || q.Get("client") != "lisimflixtest" || q.Get("clientver") != "1" || q.Get("protover") != "1" {
		f.t.Errorf("bad query %q", r.URL.RawQuery)
	}
	aid := q.Get("aid")
	f.mu.Lock()
	st, file := f.status[aid], f.files[aid]
	f.mu.Unlock()
	if st != 0 {
		w.WriteHeader(st)
		return
	}
	if file == "" {
		file = "anime_" + aid + ".xml"
	}
	body, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join("testdata", file))
	if err != nil {
		body, _ = os.ReadFile("testdata/error_notfound.xml")
	}
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/xml")
	w.Header().Set("Content-Encoding", "gzip")
	zw := gzip.NewWriter(w)
	zw.Write(body)
	zw.Close()
}

func testConfig(base string) Config {
	return Config{Client: "lisimflixtest", ClientVer: "1", BaseURL: base}.WithDefaults()
}

func newTestClient(t *testing.T, fake *fakeAniDB, cfg Config, clock *httpctest.Clock, d *db.DB) *Client {
	t.Helper()
	srv := httptest.NewServer(fake)
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	if cfg.BaseURL == "" || cfg.BaseURL == DefaultBaseURL {
		cfg.BaseURL = srv.URL
	}
	cache := animecache.New(d)
	cache.Now = clock.Now
	return NewClient(context.Background(), cfg, clock.Client(cfg.WithDefaults().Interval), cache, d, clock.Now)
}

func TestAnimeParsesAndCaches(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()

	a, err := c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if a.ID != 9541 || a.EpisodeCount != 25 || a.StartDate != "2013-04-07" || len(a.Titles) != 5 || len(a.Episodes) != 7 {
		t.Fatalf("parsed %+v", a)
	}
	if a.Titles[1].Lang != "en" || a.Titles[1].Type != "official" || a.Titles[1].Value != "Attack on Titan" {
		t.Fatalf("xml:lang not read: %+v", a.Titles[1])
	}
	if a.Ratings.Permanent.Float() != 8.16 || a.Picture != "350497.jpg" || a.Resources[1].Type != 2 || a.Resources[1].Identifiers[0] != "16498" {
		t.Fatalf("ratings/picture/resources: %+v", a)
	}
	if !a.Tags[2].LocalSpoiler || a.Tags[0].Weight != 600 {
		t.Fatalf("tags: %+v", a.Tags)
	}
	keys := [][3]int{}
	for _, e := range a.Episodes {
		s, n, ok := e.Key()
		k := [3]int{s, n, 0}
		if ok {
			k[2] = 1
		}
		keys = append(keys, k)
	}
	want := [][3]int{{1, 1, 1}, {1, 2, 1}, {1, 25, 1}, {0, 1, 1}, {0, 7, 1}, {0, 0, 0}, {0, 201, 1}}
	if !slices.Equal(keys, want) {
		t.Fatalf("episode keys %v, want %v", keys, want)
	}
	if got := a.Episodes[0].BestTitle(); got != "To You, in 2000 Years: The Fall of Shiganshina, Part 1" {
		t.Fatalf("BestTitle = %q", got)
	}
	if got := a.Episodes[4].BestTitle(); got != "Chuugakkou Recap" {
		t.Fatalf("BestTitle fallback = %q", got)
	}

	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 9541); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("same aid requested twice inside the TTL: hits=%d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestAnimeSpacesRequests(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	c.Anime(ctx, 10944)
	if !slices.Contains(clock.Slept(), DefaultInterval) {
		t.Fatalf("no %v gap between AniDB requests: %v", DefaultInterval, clock.Slept())
	}
}

func TestAnimeTTL(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	c.Anime(ctx, 9541) // ended in 2013 → 7 day TTL
	clock.Advance(25 * time.Hour)
	c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	if fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("finished anime refetched after a day: %d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
	clock.Advance(7 * 24 * time.Hour)
	c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	if fake.hits.Load() != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expired anime not refetched: %d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestNotFoundIsCachedNegative(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 1); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNotFound) {
			t.Fatalf("call %d: %v", i, err)
		}
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("not-found aid requested again: hits=%d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestBanStopsRequestsServesStaleAndPersists(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	d := dbtest.Open(t)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, d)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 9541); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	clock.Advance(8 * 24 * time.Hour) // cache now stale
	fake.set("9541", "error_banned.xml")
	fake.set("10944", "error_banned.xml")

	a, err := c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	if err != nil || a.ID != 9541 {
		t.Fatalf("stale copy must be served during a ban: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 10944); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrRateLimited) {
		t.Fatalf("uncached aid during ban: %v", err)
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("requests sent while banned: hits=%d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
	if v, ok, _ := d.GetSetting(ctx, SettingBannedUntil); !ok || v == "" {
		t.Fatal("ban not persisted")
	}
	if st := c.Status(); !st.Enabled || !strings.Contains(st.Reason, "ban") {
		t.Fatalf("status %+v", st)
	}

	// A restarted server (new client, same DB) is still banned.
	again := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, d)
	if _, err := again.Anime(ctx, 10944); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrRateLimited) {
		t.Fatalf("ban forgotten after restart: %v", err)
	}
	// After 24 h the ban is over.
	clock.Advance(25 * time.Hour)
	fake.set("10944", "anime_10944.xml")
	if _, err := again.Anime(ctx, 10944); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("after ban: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestClientErrorDisables(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	fake.set("9541", "error_client.xml")
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 9541); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrDisabled) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	st := c.Status()
	if st.Enabled || !strings.Contains(st.Reason, "client version missing or invalid") || c.Enabled() {
		t.Fatalf("status %+v", st)
	}
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 10944); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrDisabled) || fake.hits.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("disabled client still requested: %v hits=%d", err, fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestMissingConfigIsDisabledWithoutRequests(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, Config{}.WithDefaults(), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	if c.Enabled() || !strings.Contains(c.Status().Reason, "LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT") {
		t.Fatalf("status %+v", c.Status())
	}
	if _, err := c.Anime(context.Background(), 9541); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrDisabled) || fake.hits.Load() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("err=%v hits=%d", err, fake.hits.Load())
	}
}

func TestDailyCap(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	cfg := testConfig("")
	cfg.DailyCap = 1
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, cfg, clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 10944); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrRateLimited) {
		t.Fatalf("cap not enforced: %v", err)
	}
	clock.Advance(24 * time.Hour)
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 10944); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("cap must reset the next UTC day: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestServerErrorFallsBackToStale(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	c := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, dbtest.Open(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	c.Anime(ctx, 9541)
	clock.Advance(8 * 24 * time.Hour)
	fake.mu.Lock()
	fake.status["9541"] = http.StatusServiceUnavailable
	fake.mu.Unlock()
	if a, err := c.Anime(ctx, 9541); err != nil || a.ID != 9541 {
		t.Fatalf("stale fallback: %v", err)
	}
	fake.mu.Lock()
	fake.status["10944"] = http.StatusServiceUnavailable
	fake.mu.Unlock()
	if _, err := c.Anime(ctx, 10944); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("no cache and AniDB down must be an error")
	}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/config.go`:
```go
// Package anidb is the AniDB provider: HTTP API client, titles index and the
// metadata.Provider that builds TVDB-layout episode lists.
//
// AniDB rules (wiki.anidb.net/HTTP_API_Definition): registered client name and
// version required; at most one request every 2 seconds; cache heavily and
// never re-request the same data within a day, or the IP gets banned.
package anidb

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

const (
	DefaultBaseURL     = "http://api.anidb.net:9001/httpapi"
	DefaultTitlesURL   = "https://anidb.net/api/anime-titles.xml.gz"
	MinInterval        = 2 * time.Second
	DefaultInterval    = 2500 * time.Millisecond
	DefaultDailyCap    = 1000
	SettingBannedUntil = "anime.anidb.banned_until"
)

// Config is the AniDB client registration and limits.
type Config struct {
	Client    string // registered client name, lowercase
	ClientVer string // registered client version, a whole number
	BaseURL   string
	TitlesURL string
	Interval  time.Duration // gap between requests, never below MinInterval
	DailyCap  int           // max requests per UTC day
}

var clientName = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{1,63}$`)

// WithDefaults fills empty fields and enforces the 2 s floor.
func (c Config) WithDefaults() Config {
	if c.BaseURL == "" {
		c.BaseURL = DefaultBaseURL
	}
	if c.TitlesURL == "" {
		c.TitlesURL = DefaultTitlesURL
	}
	if c.Interval == 0 {
		c.Interval = DefaultInterval
	}
	if c.Interval < MinInterval {
		c.Interval = MinInterval
	}
	if c.DailyCap <= 0 {
		c.DailyCap = DefaultDailyCap
	}
	return c
}

// Validate explains, for admins, what is wrong with the registration.
func (c Config) Validate() error {
	if c.Client == "" || c.ClientVer == "" {
		return errors.New("AniDB needs a registered HTTP API client: register one on anidb.net, then set " +
			"LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT and LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER (or the settings anime.anidb.client and anime.anidb.clientver)")
	}
	if !clientName.MatchString(c.Client) {
		return fmt.Errorf("LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT must be the lowercase client name registered on anidb.net, got %q", c.Client)
	}
	if n, err := strconv.Atoi(c.ClientVer); err != nil || n < 1 {
		return fmt.Errorf("LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER must be a whole number (the registered client version), got %q", c.ClientVer)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/xml.go`:
```go
package anidb

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/xml"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// Anime is the <anime> document of request=anime.
type Anime struct {
	XMLName      xml.Name   `xml:"anime"`
	ID           int        `xml:"id,attr"`
	Restricted   bool       `xml:"restricted,attr"`
	Type         string     `xml:"type"`
	EpisodeCount int        `xml:"episodecount"`
	StartDate    string     `xml:"startdate"`
	EndDate      string     `xml:"enddate"`
	Titles       []Title    `xml:"titles>title"`
	Related      []Related  `xml:"relatedanime>anime"`
	Creators     []Creator  `xml:"creators>name"`
	Description  string     `xml:"description"`
	Ratings      Ratings    `xml:"ratings"`
	Picture      string     `xml:"picture"`
	Resources    []Resource `xml:"resources>resource"`
	Tags         []Tag      `xml:"tags>tag"`
	Episodes     []Episode  `xml:"episodes>episode"`
}

// Title is a title with xml:lang and type (main, official, synonym/syn, short, kana, card).
type Title struct {
	Lang  string `xml:"lang,attr"`
	Type  string `xml:"type,attr"`
	Value string `xml:",chardata"`
}

type Related struct {
	ID    int    `xml:"id,attr"`
	Type  string `xml:"type,attr"`
	Title string `xml:",chardata"`
}

type Creator struct {
	ID   int    `xml:"id,attr"`
	Type string `xml:"type,attr"` // "Animation Work" = studio
	Name string `xml:",chardata"`
}

type Ratings struct {
	Permanent Rating `xml:"permanent"`
	Temporary Rating `xml:"temporary"`
}

type Rating struct {
	Count int    `xml:"count,attr"`
	Value string `xml:",chardata"`
}

// Float is the rating value, 0 when missing.
func (r Rating) Float() float64 {
	f, _ := strconv.ParseFloat(strings.TrimSpace(r.Value), 64)
	return f
}

// Resource links to other sites; type 2 is MyAnimeList, type 1 Anime News Network.
type Resource struct {
	Type        int      `xml:"type,attr"`
	Identifiers []string `xml:"externalentity>identifier"`
}

type Tag struct {
	ID            int    `xml:"id,attr"`
	Weight        int    `xml:"weight,attr"` // 0..600
	LocalSpoiler  bool   `xml:"localspoiler,attr"`
	GlobalSpoiler bool   `xml:"globalspoiler,attr"`
	Name          string `xml:"name"`
}

type Episode struct {
	ID      int     `xml:"id,attr"`
	EpNo    EpNo    `xml:"epno"`
	Length  int     `xml:"length"`
	AirDate string  `xml:"airdate"`
	Titles  []Title `xml:"title"`
	Summary string  `xml:"summary"`
}

// EpNo type: 1 regular, 2 special (S), 3 credits (C), 4 trailer (T), 5 parody (P), 6 other (O).
type EpNo struct {
	Type  int    `xml:"type,attr"`
	Value string `xml:",chardata"`
}

// Key converts the episode number to anime-lists numbering.
func (e Episode) Key() (anidbSeason, n int, ok bool) {
	num, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimLeft(strings.TrimSpace(e.EpNo.Value), "SCTPO"))
	if err != nil || num < 1 {
		return 0, 0, false
	}
	switch e.EpNo.Type {
	case 1:
		return 1, num, true
	case 2:
		return 0, num, true
	case 4:
		return 0, 200 + num, true
	case 6:
		return 0, 400 + num, true
	}
	return 0, 0, false
}

// BestTitle prefers English, then romaji, then Japanese, then any title.
func (e Episode) BestTitle() string {
	for _, lang := range []string{"en", "x-jat", "ja"} {
		for _, t := range e.Titles {
			if t.Lang == lang && strings.TrimSpace(t.Value) != "" {
				return strings.TrimSpace(t.Value)
			}
		}
	}
	if len(e.Titles) > 0 {
		return strings.TrimSpace(e.Titles[0].Value)
	}
	return ""
}

type apiError struct {
	Code    string `xml:"code,attr"`
	Message string `xml:",chardata"`
}

// parseError reports whether body is an AniDB <error> document.
func parseError(body []byte) (apiError, bool) {
	dec := xml.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(body))
	for {
		tok, err := dec.Token()
		if err != nil {
			return apiError{}, false
		}
		se, ok := tok.(xml.StartElement)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if se.Name.Local != "error" {
			return apiError{}, false
		}
		var e apiError
		if err := dec.DecodeElement(&e, &se); err != nil {
			return apiError{}, false
		}
		e.Message = strings.TrimSpace(e.Message)
		return e, true
	}
}

func parseAnime(body []byte) (Anime, error) {
	var a Anime
	if err := xml.Unmarshal(body, &a); err != nil {
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("parse anidb anime: %w", err)
	}
	if a.ID <= 0 {
		return Anime{}, errors.New("parse anidb anime: missing id")
	}
	return a, nil
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/client.go`:
```go
package anidb

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc"
)

const (
	cacheProvider = "anidb"
	banPause      = 24 * time.Hour
	shortTTL      = 24 * time.Hour
	longTTL       = 7 * 24 * time.Hour
)

// Client fetches request=anime documents with AniDB's rules applied.
type Client struct {
	cfg   Config
	http  *httpc.Client
	cache *animecache.Cache
	db    *db.DB
	now   func() time.Time

	mu          sync.Mutex
	disabled    string
	bannedUntil time.Time
	day         string
	count       int
}

// NewClient builds the client and restores a persisted ban.
func NewClient(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, hc *httpc.Client, cache *animecache.Cache, d *db.DB, now func() time.Time) *Client {
	c := &Client{cfg: cfg.WithDefaults(), http: hc, cache: cache, db: d, now: now}
	if err := c.cfg.Validate(); err != nil {
		c.disabled = err.Error()
	}
	if v, ok, err := d.GetSetting(ctx, SettingBannedUntil); err == nil && ok {
		if t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, v); err == nil {
			c.bannedUntil = t
		}
	}
	return c
}

// Enabled is false when the client is not (or no longer) usable.
func (c *Client) Enabled() bool {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.disabled == ""
}

// Status is shown on the admin anime status page.
func (c *Client) Status() anime.Status {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	switch {
	case c.disabled != "":
		return anime.Status{Name: "anidb", Enabled: false, Reason: c.disabled}
	case c.now().Before(c.bannedUntil):
		return anime.Status{Name: "anidb", Enabled: true, Reason: "AniDB reported a ban; requests are paused until " +
			c.bannedUntil.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339) + " (cached data is still used)"}
	}
	return anime.Status{Name: "anidb", Enabled: true}
}

// Anime returns the anime document for aid. See plan 1E Task 10 for the order of checks.
func (c *Client) Anime(ctx context.Context, aid int) (Anime, error) {
	key := strconv.Itoa(aid)
	ent, cached, err := c.cache.Get(ctx, cacheProvider, key)
	if err != nil {
		return Anime{}, err
	}
	if cached && ent.Fresh(c.now()) {
		return decodeCached(ent.Body, aid)
	}
	if err := c.allow(); err != nil {
		if cached {
			return decodeCached(ent.Body, aid)
		}
		return Anime{}, err
	}
	resp, err := c.http.Do(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) (*http.Request, error) {
		q := url.Values{
			"request": {"anime"}, "client": {c.cfg.Client}, "clientver": {c.cfg.ClientVer},
			"protover": {"1"}, "aid": {key},
		}
		return http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, c.cfg.BaseURL+"?"+q.Encode(), nil)
	})
	if err == nil && resp.Status != http.StatusOK {
		err = fmt.Errorf("unexpected status %d", resp.Status)
	}
	if err != nil {
		if cached && ctx.Err() == nil {
			return decodeCached(ent.Body, aid)
		}
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("anidb anime %d: %w", aid, err)
	}
	if apiErr, ok := parseError(resp.Body); ok {
		return c.handleError(ctx, aid, apiErr, ent, cached, resp.Body)
	}
	a, err := parseAnime(resp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("anidb anime %d: %w", aid, err)
	}
	if err := c.cache.Put(ctx, cacheProvider, key, resp.Body, ttlFor(a, c.now())); err != nil {
		return Anime{}, err
	}
	return a, nil
}

func decodeCached(body []byte, aid int) (Anime, error) {
	if e, ok := parseError(body); ok {
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("anidb anime %d: %s: %w", aid, e.Message, anime.ErrNotFound)
	}
	return parseAnime(body)
}

// allow checks disabled/ban/daily cap and counts the request.
func (c *Client) allow() error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if c.disabled != "" {
		return fmt.Errorf("anidb: %s: %w", c.disabled, anime.ErrDisabled)
	}
	now := c.now()
	if now.Before(c.bannedUntil) {
		return fmt.Errorf("anidb: banned until %s: %w", c.bannedUntil.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339), anime.ErrRateLimited)
	}
	day := now.UTC().Format("2006-01-02")
	if day != c.day {
		c.day, c.count = day, 0
	}
	if c.count >= c.cfg.DailyCap {
		return fmt.Errorf("anidb: daily cap of %d requests reached: %w", c.cfg.DailyCap, anime.ErrRateLimited)
	}
	c.count++
	return nil
}

func (c *Client) handleError(ctx context.Context, aid int, e apiError, ent animecache.Entry, cached bool, body []byte) (Anime, error) {
	msg := strings.ToLower(e.Message)
	switch {
	case strings.Contains(msg, "banned"):
		until := c.now().Add(banPause)
		c.mu.Lock()
		c.bannedUntil = until
		c.mu.Unlock()
		if err := c.db.SetSetting(ctx, SettingBannedUntil, until.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)); err != nil {
			return Anime{}, err
		}
		if cached {
			return decodeCached(ent.Body, aid)
		}
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("anidb: banned (%s): %w", e.Message, anime.ErrRateLimited)
	case strings.Contains(msg, "not found"):
		if err := c.cache.Put(ctx, cacheProvider, strconv.Itoa(aid), body, shortTTL); err != nil {
			return Anime{}, err
		}
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("anidb anime %d: %w", aid, anime.ErrNotFound)
	default:
		reason := fmt.Sprintf("AniDB rejected the client: %s (code %s). Check LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT and LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER.", e.Message, e.Code)
		c.mu.Lock()
		c.disabled = reason
		c.mu.Unlock()
		return Anime{}, fmt.Errorf("anidb: %s: %w", e.Message, anime.ErrDisabled)
	}
}

// ttlFor keeps finished anime for a week, everything else for a day.
func ttlFor(a Anime, now time.Time) time.Duration {
	if end, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02", strings.TrimSpace(a.EndDate)); err == nil && end.Before(now.AddDate(0, 0, -30)) {
		return longTTL
	}
	return shortTTL
}
```

### Task 11: AniDB titles index (daily dump, local title search)

AniDB has no search endpoint; the documented way is the daily `anime-titles.xml.gz` dump (about
17 000 anime, every title type and language). It is loaded into `anime_anidb_titles` with a
normalized column so searches match romaji, English, Japanese, synonyms and short titles without
any network call. The dump is a `Dataset` with TTL 24 h **and** MinRetry 24 h, so even failed
downloads are not retried the same day.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/db/migrations/20261025130200_anime_anidb_titles.sql`
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/titles.go`
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/testdata/anime-titles.xml`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/titles_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `anime.Normalize`, `anime.Similarity`, `animecache.Dataset`, `httpc.Gunzip`, `Title` (Task 10).
- Produces:
  - Table `anime_anidb_titles(aid, lang, type, title, norm)`.
  - `anidb.NewTitleIndex(d *db.DB) *TitleIndex`
  - `(*TitleIndex).Dataset(url string) animecache.Dataset` (name `anidb-titles`, file `anime-titles.xml`, TTL 24 h, MinRetry 24 h; accepts gzip or plain XML)
  - `anidb.ParseTitles(r io.Reader, fn func(aid int, titles []Title) error) error`
  - `type anidb.TitleHit struct{ AID int; Title string; Score float64 }` (`Title` = English official, else main)
  - `(*TitleIndex).Search(ctx, query string, limit int) ([]TitleHit, error)` (best `anime.Similarity` over all titles of each candidate, highest first, ties by aid)
  - `(*TitleIndex).MainTitle(ctx, aid int) (string, error)` (English official, else main, else "")

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the migration and fixture**

`server/internal/db/migrations/20261025130200_anime_anidb_titles.sql`:
```sql
-- AniDB anime-titles.xml.gz dump (plan 1E), replaced at most once a day.
-- norm = anime.Normalize(title), used for matching.
CREATE TABLE anime_anidb_titles (
    aid   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    lang  TEXT    NOT NULL,
    type  TEXT    NOT NULL,
    title TEXT    NOT NULL,
    norm  TEXT    NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE INDEX anime_anidb_titles_norm ON anime_anidb_titles(norm);
CREATE INDEX anime_anidb_titles_aid  ON anime_anidb_titles(aid);
```

```go
package anidb

import (
	"bytes"
	"compress/gzip"
	"context"
	"os"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
)

// loadedTitles returns an index filled from testdata/anime-titles.xml.
func loadedTitles(t *testing.T) *TitleIndex {
	t.Helper()
	ti := NewTitleIndex(dbtest.Open(t))
	body, err := os.ReadFile("testdata/anime-titles.xml")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	n, err := ti.Dataset(DefaultTitlesURL).Load(context.Background(), body)
	if err != nil || n != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("loaded %d anime, err %v", n, err)
	}
	return ti
}

func TestTitlesDatasetRules(t *testing.T) {
	ds := NewTitleIndex(dbtest.Open(t)).Dataset("https://example.invalid/t.xml.gz")
	if ds.Name != "anidb-titles" || ds.File != "anime-titles.xml" || ds.TTL != 24*time.Hour || ds.MinRetry != 24*time.Hour {
		t.Fatalf("dataset %+v", ds)
	}
}

func TestTitlesLoadAcceptsGzipAndRejectsEmpty(t *testing.T) {
	ti := NewTitleIndex(dbtest.Open(t))
	body, _ := os.ReadFile("testdata/anime-titles.xml")
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	zw := gzip.NewWriter(&buf)
	zw.Write(body)
	zw.Close()
	ctx := context.Background()
	if n, err := ti.Dataset("").Load(ctx, buf.Bytes()); err != nil || n != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("gzip load: %d %v", n, err)
	}
	if _, err := ti.Dataset("").Load(ctx, []byte(`<animetitles></animetitles>`)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("empty dump must be rejected")
	}
	if hits, _ := ti.Search(ctx, "Attack on Titan", 5); len(hits) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("a rejected dump must not wipe the index")
	}
}

func TestTitleSearch(t *testing.T) {
	ti := loadedTitles(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	cases := []struct {
		query   string
		wantAID int
		exact   bool
	}{
		{"Attack on Titan", 9541, true},
		{"attack on titan season 3 part 2", 14444, true},
		{"Attack on Titan Season 3", 13241, true},
		{"Shingeki no Kyojin", 9541, true},
		{"SnK", 9541, true},                // short title
		{"Advancing Giants", 9541, true},   // synonym
		{"進撃の巨人", 9541, true},             // Japanese
		{"Patema Inverted", 8857, true},
		{"sakasama no patema", 8857, true},
		{"Attack on Titan Season Two", 10944, false},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		hits, err := ti.Search(ctx, c.query, 5)
		if err != nil || len(hits) == 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%q: no hits (%v)", c.query, err)
		}
		if hits[0].AID != c.wantAID {
			t.Errorf("%q: best aid %d (%+v), want %d", c.query, hits[0].AID, hits, c.wantAID)
		}
		if c.exact && hits[0].Score != 1 {
			t.Errorf("%q: exact title scored %v", c.query, hits[0].Score)
		}
	}
	hits, _ := ti.Search(ctx, "Attack on Titan Season 3", 2)
	// "Attack on Titan Season 2" (10944) is closer to this query than "... Season 3 Part 2".
	if len(hits) != 2 || hits[1].AID != 10944 || hits[1].Score >= 1 {
		t.Fatalf("limit/order: %+v", hits)
	}
	if hits[0].Title != "Attack on Titan Season 3" {
		t.Fatalf("hit title should be the English official title: %q", hits[0].Title)
	}
	if hits, _ := ti.Search(ctx, "zzzz nothing", 5); len(hits) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected hits %+v", hits)
	}
	if hits, _ := ti.Search(ctx, "  !! ", 5); len(hits) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("empty normalized query must return nothing")
	}
}

func TestMainTitle(t *testing.T) {
	ti := loadedTitles(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	for aid, want := range map[int]string{9541: "Attack on Titan", 13241: "Attack on Titan Season 3", 424242: ""} {
		if got, err := ti.MainTitle(ctx, aid); err != nil || got != want {
			t.Errorf("MainTitle(%d) = %q %v, want %q", aid, got, err, want)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/titles.go`:
```go
package anidb

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/xml"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc"
)

// TitleIndex is the local copy of AniDB's titles dump.
type TitleIndex struct{ db *db.DB }

func NewTitleIndex(d *db.DB) *TitleIndex { return &TitleIndex{db: d} }

// TitleHit is one search result.
type TitleHit struct {
	AID   int
	Title string
	Score float64
}

// Dataset is the daily dump download. AniDB allows one download per day.
func (t *TitleIndex) Dataset(url string) animecache.Dataset {
	return animecache.Dataset{
		Name: "anidb-titles", URL: url, File: "anime-titles.xml",
		TTL: 24 * time.Hour, MinRetry: 24 * time.Hour,
		Load: t.load,
	}
}

// ParseTitles streams the dump, calling fn once per <anime>.
func ParseTitles(r io.Reader, fn func(aid int, titles []Title) error) error {
	dec := xml.NewDecoder(r)
	for {
		tok, err := dec.Token()
		if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
			return nil
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("anidb titles: %w", err)
		}
		se, ok := tok.(xml.StartElement)
		if !ok || se.Name.Local != "anime" {
			continue
		}
		var a struct {
			AID    int     `xml:"aid,attr"`
			Titles []Title `xml:"title"`
		}
		if err := dec.DecodeElement(&a, &se); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("anidb titles: %w", err)
		}
		if a.AID <= 0 {
			continue
		}
		if err := fn(a.AID, a.Titles); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
}

func (t *TitleIndex) load(ctx context.Context, body []byte) (int, error) {
	body, err := httpc.Gunzip(body)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	tx, err := t.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer tx.Rollback()
	if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM anime_anidb_titles`); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("clear anidb titles: %w", err)
	}
	stmt, err := tx.PrepareContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO anime_anidb_titles(aid, lang, type, title, norm) VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer stmt.Close()
	n := 0
	err = ParseTitles(bytes.NewReader(body), func(aid int, titles []Title) error {
		for _, tt := range titles {
			v := strings.TrimSpace(tt.Value)
			norm := anime.Normalize(v)
			if norm == "" {
				continue
			}
			if _, err := stmt.ExecContext(ctx, aid, tt.Lang, tt.Type, v, norm); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("insert anidb title %d: %w", aid, err)
			}
		}
		n++
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return 0, errors.New("anidb titles: dump has no anime")
	}
	return n, tx.Commit()
}

// Search finds anime whose titles match query in any language or type.
func (t *TitleIndex) Search(ctx context.Context, query string, limit int) ([]TitleHit, error) {
	norm := anime.Normalize(query)
	if norm == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	aids := map[int]bool{}
	collect := func(q string, args ...any) error {
		rows, err := t.db.QueryContext(ctx, q, args...)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("anidb title search: %w", err)
		}
		defer rows.Close()
		for rows.Next() {
			var aid int
			if err := rows.Scan(&aid); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			aids[aid] = true
		}
		return rows.Err()
	}
	if err := collect(`SELECT DISTINCT aid FROM anime_anidb_titles WHERE norm = ? OR norm LIKE ? LIMIT 200`, norm, norm+"%"); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if tok := longestToken(norm); len([]rune(tok)) >= 3 {
		if err := collect(`SELECT DISTINCT aid FROM anime_anidb_titles WHERE norm LIKE ? LIMIT 200`, "%"+tok+"%"); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	if len(aids) == 0 {
		return nil, nil
	}
	args := make([]any, 0, len(aids))
	for aid := range aids {
		args = append(args, aid)
	}
	rows, err := t.db.QueryContext(ctx, `SELECT aid, lang, type, title FROM anime_anidb_titles WHERE aid IN (?`+
		strings.Repeat(",?", len(args)-1)+`)`, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anidb title search: %w", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	type acc struct {
		hit      TitleHit
		main, en string
	}
	best := map[int]*acc{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var aid int
		var lang, typ, title string
		if err := rows.Scan(&aid, &lang, &typ, &title); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		a := best[aid]
		if a == nil {
			a = &acc{hit: TitleHit{AID: aid}}
			best[aid] = a
		}
		if s := anime.Similarity(query, title); s > a.hit.Score {
			a.hit.Score = s
		}
		if typ == "main" && a.main == "" {
			a.main = title
		}
		if typ == "official" && lang == "en" && a.en == "" {
			a.en = title
		}
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	hits := make([]TitleHit, 0, len(best))
	for _, a := range best {
		a.hit.Title = a.en
		if a.hit.Title == "" {
			a.hit.Title = a.main
		}
		hits = append(hits, a.hit)
	}
	sort.Slice(hits, func(i, j int) bool {
		if hits[i].Score != hits[j].Score {
			return hits[i].Score > hits[j].Score
		}
		return hits[i].AID < hits[j].AID
	})
	if limit > 0 && len(hits) > limit {
		hits = hits[:limit]
	}
	return hits, nil
}

// MainTitle is the English official title of aid, else its main title, else "".
func (t *TitleIndex) MainTitle(ctx context.Context, aid int) (string, error) {
	var title string
	err := t.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT title FROM anime_anidb_titles WHERE aid = ? AND
		((type = 'official' AND lang = 'en') OR type = 'main')
		ORDER BY (type = 'official') DESC LIMIT 1`, aid).Scan(&title)
	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("anidb main title %d: %w", aid, err)
	}
	return title, nil
}

func longestToken(norm string) string {
	best := ""
	for _, tok := range strings.Fields(norm) {
		if len([]rune(tok)) > len([]rune(best)) {
			best = tok
		}
	}
	return best
}
```

### Task 12: AniDB provider (search, record, TVDB-layout seasons)

`Fetch(show)` builds the whole TVDB series' episode list (see "Design decisions"): every
anime-lists entry with the same TVDB id and a default season is fetched (cache first), each AniDB
episode is placed with `Entry.ToTVDB`, and absolute numbers are computed from AniDB
`episodecount`s. Specials/OVAs land in season 0, credits and parodies are skipped, trailers only
appear when an explicit mapping names them. Entries without a TVDB series get a plain layout
(season 1 = regular episodes with absolute = number, season 0 = specials).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/record.go`, `anidb/provider.go`
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/testdata/anime_9826.xml`, `testdata/anime_13241.xml`,
  `testdata/anime_14444.xml`, `testdata/anime_8857.xml`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/provider_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Client`, `TitleIndex` (Tasks 10-11), `epmap.Entry`, `epmap.Series`, `epmap.SeasonAbsolute`,
  `animelists.Store` (tests), `metadata.*`, `anime.*`.
- Produces:
  - `anidb.Name = "anidb"`, `anidb.ImageBaseURL = "https://cdn.anidb.net/images/main/"`
  - `type anidb.Lists interface{ Entry(ctx, aid int) (epmap.Entry, bool, error); EntriesByTVDB(ctx, tvdbID int) (epmap.Series, error); MediaType(ctx, aid int) (string, error) }` (implemented by `*animelists.Store`)
  - `anidb.NewProvider(c *Client, t *TitleIndex, l Lists, log *slog.Logger) *Provider`
  - `(*Provider)` implements `metadata.Provider`; `Status() anime.Status` (the client's status)
  - `Search`: known `ids["anidb"]` → one candidate (score 1, title from the index); else local
    title search, score ×0.8 when the Fribb type disagrees with the query kind (movie vs not).
  - `Fetch`: aid from `ids["anidb"]`, else the primary entry of `ids["tvdb"]`; record fields:
    `Title` English official (else main), `OriginalTitle` Japanese official, `AltTitles`, `Overview`
    (AniDB link markup and "Source:"/"Note:"/"* Based on" lines removed), `Year`/`Released` from
    `startdate`, `Studios` (creators of type "Animation Work"), `Tags` (weight ≥ 300, no spoilers,
    max 15, heaviest first), `Ratings["anidb"]` (permanent, else temporary), poster image,
    `IDs` anidb + mal (resource type 2) + tvdb (shows); `Seasons` for shows.

```xml
  </anime>
```

```xml
  </anime>
```

```go
func newTestProvider(t *testing.T, fake *fakeAniDB) *Provider {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	d := dbtest.Open(t)
	clock := httpctest.NewClock(httpctest.Epoch)
	client := newTestClient(t, fake, testConfig(""), clock, d)
	lists := animelists.NewStore(d)
	entries, _, err := animelists.ParseMaster(strings.NewReader(testMaster))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := lists.ReplaceMaster(ctx, entries); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	err = lists.ReplaceIDs(ctx, []animelists.IDRow{
		{AniDB: 8857, Type: "MOVIE", TVDBSeason: -1, TMDBSeason: -1},
		{AniDB: 9541, Type: "TV", TVDBSeason: 1, TMDBSeason: 1},
		{AniDB: 69, Type: "TV", TVDBSeason: -1, TMDBSeason: -1},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return NewProvider(client, loadedTitles(t), lists, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
}

// layoutLines renders seasons as "S3E13 abs=50 Title" lines.
func layoutLines(rec metadata.Record) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, s := range rec.Seasons {
		for _, e := range s.Episodes {
			abs := "-"
			if e.Absolute != nil {
				abs = fmt.Sprint(*e.Absolute)
			}
			if e.Season != s.Number {
				out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("BAD season %d in season %d", e.Season, s.Number))
			}
			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("S%dE%d abs=%s %s", s.Number, e.Number, abs, e.Title))
		}
	}
	return out
}

func TestFetchShowBuildsTVDBLayoutAcrossEntries(t *testing.T) {
	p := newTestProvider(t, newFake(t))
	rec, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anidb": "9541"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []string{
		"S0E1 abs=- Since That Day",
		"S0E7 abs=- Ilse's Notebook: Notes of a Scout Regiment Member",
		"S0E8 abs=- Chuugakkou Recap",
		"S1E1 abs=1 To You, in 2000 Years: The Fall of Shiganshina, Part 1",
		"S1E2 abs=2 That Day: The Fall of Shiganshina, Part 2",
		"S1E25 abs=25 Wall: Assault on Stohess, Part 3",
		"S2E1 abs=26 Beast Titan",
		"S3E1 abs=38 Smoke Signal",
		"S3E12 abs=49 Night of the Battle to Retake the Wall",
		"S3E13 abs=50 The Town Where Everything Began",
		"S3E22 abs=59 The Other Side of the Wall",
	}
	if got := layoutLines(rec); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("layout:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", strings.Join(got, "\n"), strings.Join(want, "\n"))
	}
	if rec.Seasons[0].Title != "Specials" || rec.Seasons[3].Title != "Season 3" {
		t.Fatalf("season titles %q %q", rec.Seasons[0].Title, rec.Seasons[3].Title)
	}
	ep := rec.Seasons[1].Episodes[0]
	if ep.AirDate != "2013-04-07" || ep.Overview != "Eren Yeager lives inside the walls of Shiganshina." {
		t.Fatalf("episode fields %+v", ep)
	}
	if rec.IDs["tvdb"] != "267440" || rec.IDs["anidb"] != "9541" || rec.IDs["mal"] != "16498" {
		t.Fatalf("ids %v", rec.IDs)
	}
}

func TestFetchShowMissingEntryLeavesLaterAbsolutesUnknown(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)

func TestFetchMovieAndStandaloneAndByTVDB(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	p := newTestProvider(t, fake)
	ctx := context.Background()

	mv, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindMovie, map[string]string{"anidb": "8857"})
	if err != nil || mv.Title != "Patema Inverted" || mv.Seasons != nil || mv.Ratings["anidb"] != 7.95 || mv.IDs["mal"] != "12477" {
		t.Fatalf("movie %+v err %v", mv, err)
	}
	if _, ok := mv.IDs["tvdb"]; ok {
		t.Fatal("movie must not get a tvdb id")
	}

	sa, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"anidb": "8857"})
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(layoutLines(sa), []string{"S1E1 abs=1 Complete Movie"}) {
		t.Fatalf("standalone layout %v err %v", layoutLines(sa), err)
	}

	byTVDB, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"tvdb": "267440"})
	if err != nil || byTVDB.IDs["anidb"] != "9541" {
		t.Fatalf("by tvdb: %v %v", byTVDB.IDs, err)
	}

	if _, err := p.Fetch(ctx, anime.KindShow, map[string]string{"mal": "1"}); !errors.Is(err, anime.ErrNoUsableID) {
		t.Fatalf("no usable id: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestSearch(t *testing.T) {
	fake := newFake(t)
	p := newTestProvider(t, fake)
	ctx := context.Background()
	q := func(kind, title string) metadata.Query {
		k := anime.KindShow
		if kind == "movie" {
			k = anime.KindMovie
		}
		return metadata.Query{Kind: k, LibType: anime.LibAnime, Title: title}
	}
	got, err := p.Search(ctx, q("show", "Attack on Titan Season 3 Part 2"))
	if err != nil || len(got) == 0 || got[0].IDs["anidb"] != "14444" || got[0].Score != 1 || got[0].Provider != "anidb" {
		t.Fatalf("show search %+v %v", got, err)
	}
	got, _ = p.Search(ctx, q("movie", "Patema Inverted"))
	if len(got) == 0 || got[0].IDs["anidb"] != "8857" || got[0].Score != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("movie search %+v", got)
	}
	got, _ = p.Search(ctx, q("movie", "One Piece"))
	if len(got) == 0 || got[0].IDs["anidb"] != "69" || got[0].Score != 0.8 {
		t.Fatalf("TV series searched as a movie must be penalised: %+v", got)
	}
	got, _ = p.Search(ctx, metadata.Query{Kind: anime.KindShow, LibType: anime.LibAnime, IDs: map[string]string{"anidb": "9541"}})
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].Title != "Attack on Titan" || got[0].Score != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("known id %+v", got)
	}
	if fake.hits.Load() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("search must not call AniDB: hits=%d", fake.hits.Load())
	}
	if p.Name() != "anidb" || !p.Supports(anime.KindMovie, anime.LibAnime) || !p.Status().Enabled {
		t.Fatal("name/supports/status")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/record.go`:
```go
package anidb

import (
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
)

const (
	Name         = "anidb"
	ImageBaseURL = "https://cdn.anidb.net/images/main/"
	minTagWeight = 300
	maxTags      = 15
)

// linkMarkup matches AniDB's "http://anidb.net/ch7247 [Name]" description links
// (old and new URL styles).
var linkMarkup = regexp.MustCompile(`https?://anidb\.net/[a-z]+/?\d+ \[([^\]]+)\]`)

func cleanDescription(s string) string {
	s = linkMarkup.ReplaceAllString(strings.ReplaceAll(s, "\r\n", "\n"), "$1")
	var keep []string
	for _, line := range strings.Split(s, "\n") {
		l := strings.TrimSpace(line)
		if strings.HasPrefix(l, "* Based on") || strings.HasPrefix(l, "Source:") || strings.HasPrefix(l, "Note:") {
			continue
		}
		keep = append(keep, l)
	}
	return strings.TrimSpace(strings.Join(keep, "\n"))
}

func pickTitles(ts []Title) (en, romaji, native string) {
	for _, t := range ts {
		v := strings.TrimSpace(t.Value)
		switch {
		case t.Type == "main" && romaji == "":
			romaji = v
		case t.Type == "official" && t.Lang == "en" && en == "":
			en = v
		case t.Type == "official" && t.Lang == "ja" && native == "":
			native = v
		}
	}
	return en, romaji, native
}

func parseDate(s string) (int, string) {
	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
	year := 0
	if len(s) >= 4 {
		year, _ = strconv.Atoi(s[:4])
	}
	if len(s) == 10 {
		return year, s
	}
	return year, ""
}

func pickTags(tags []Tag) []string {
	sorted := append([]Tag(nil), tags...)
	sort.SliceStable(sorted, func(i, j int) bool { return sorted[i].Weight > sorted[j].Weight })
	var out []string
	for _, t := range sorted {
		if t.Weight < minTagWeight || t.LocalSpoiler || t.GlobalSpoiler || strings.TrimSpace(t.Name) == "" {
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, strings.TrimSpace(t.Name))
		if len(out) == maxTags {
			break
		}
	}
	return out
}

func toRecord(a Anime) metadata.Record {
	en, romaji, native := pickTitles(a.Titles)
	title := en
	if title == "" {
		title = romaji
	}
	rec := metadata.Record{
		Provider:      Name,
		IDs:           map[string]string{"anidb": strconv.Itoa(a.ID)},
		Title:         title,
		OriginalTitle: native,
		AltTitles:     anime.AltTitles(en, romaji, native),
		Overview:      cleanDescription(a.Description),
		Ratings:       map[string]float64{},
		Tags:          pickTags(a.Tags),
	}
	rec.Year, rec.Released = parseDate(a.StartDate)
	for _, r := range a.Resources {
		if r.Type == 2 && len(r.Identifiers) > 0 {
			if n, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(r.Identifiers[0])); err == nil {
				anime.SetID(rec.IDs, "mal", n)
			}
		}
	}
	for _, c := range a.Creators {
		if c.Type == "Animation Work" && strings.TrimSpace(c.Name) != "" {
			rec.Studios = append(rec.Studios, strings.TrimSpace(c.Name))
		}
	}
	score := a.Ratings.Permanent.Float()
	if score == 0 {
		score = a.Ratings.Temporary.Float()
	}
	if score > 0 {
		rec.Ratings["anidb"] = score
	}
	if pic := strings.TrimSpace(a.Picture); pic != "" {
		rec.Images = []metadata.Image{{Type: "poster", URL: ImageBaseURL + pic}}
	}
	return rec
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb/provider.go`:
```go
package anidb

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/media"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap"
)

// Lists is what the provider needs from the anime-lists store.
type Lists interface {
	Entry(ctx context.Context, aid int) (epmap.Entry, bool, error)
	EntriesByTVDB(ctx context.Context, tvdbID int) (epmap.Series, error)
	MediaType(ctx context.Context, aid int) (string, error)
}

// Provider implements metadata.Provider for AniDB.
type Provider struct {
	client *Client
	titles *TitleIndex
	lists  Lists
	log    *slog.Logger
}

var _ metadata.Provider = (*Provider)(nil)

func NewProvider(c *Client, t *TitleIndex, l Lists, log *slog.Logger) *Provider {
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	}
	return &Provider{client: c, titles: t, lists: l, log: log}
}

func (p *Provider) Name() string                                    { return Name }
func (p *Provider) Supports(kind media.Kind, lt library.Type) bool { return anime.Supports(kind, lt) }
func (p *Provider) Status() anime.Status                            { return p.client.Status() }

// Search never calls AniDB: it uses the local titles index.
func (p *Provider) Search(ctx context.Context, q metadata.Query) ([]metadata.Candidate, error) {
	if aid, ok := anime.IDInt(q.IDs, "anidb"); ok {
		title, err := p.titles.MainTitle(ctx, aid)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return []metadata.Candidate{{Provider: Name, IDs: map[string]string{"anidb": strconv.Itoa(aid)}, Title: title, Score: 1}}, nil
	}
	hits, err := p.titles.Search(ctx, q.Title, 10)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]metadata.Candidate, 0, len(hits))
	for _, h := range hits {
		score := h.Score
		typ, err := p.lists.MediaType(ctx, h.AID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		switch {
		case q.Kind == anime.KindMovie && typ != "" && typ != "MOVIE":
			score *= 0.8
		case q.Kind == anime.KindShow && typ == "MOVIE":
			score *= 0.8
		}
		out = append(out, metadata.Candidate{Provider: Name, IDs: map[string]string{"anidb": strconv.Itoa(h.AID)}, Title: h.Title, Score: score})
	}
	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Score > out[j].Score })
	return out, nil
}

// Fetch returns the AniDB record; shows also get TVDB-layout seasons.
func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, kind media.Kind, ids map[string]string) (metadata.Record, error) {
	aid, err := p.resolveAID(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return metadata.Record{}, err
	}
	a, err := p.client.Anime(ctx, aid)
	if err != nil {
		return metadata.Record{}, err
	}
	rec := toRecord(a)
	if kind == anime.KindMovie {
		return rec, nil
	}
	seasons, tvdb, err := p.seasons(ctx, a)
	if err != nil {
		return metadata.Record{}, err
	}
	rec.Seasons = seasons
	anime.SetID(rec.IDs, "tvdb", tvdb)
	return rec, nil
}

func (p *Provider) resolveAID(ctx context.Context, ids map[string]string) (int, error) {
	if aid, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "anidb"); ok {
		return aid, nil
	}
	if tvdb, ok := anime.IDInt(ids, "tvdb"); ok {
		series, err := p.lists.EntriesByTVDB(ctx, tvdb)
		if err != nil {
			return 0, err
		}
		if e, ok := series.Primary(); ok {
			return e.AniDBID, nil
		}
	}
	return 0, fmt.Errorf("anidb: no anidb id: %w", anime.ErrNoUsableID)
}

// regular reports whether an entry's regular episodes are in a TVDB season >= 1.
func regular(e epmap.Entry) bool { return e.Absolute || e.DefaultSeason >= 1 }

// members are the entries that make up the TVDB series, in absolute order:
// regular entries by (season, offset, aid), then season-0 entries by aid.
func members(series epmap.Series) []epmap.Entry {
	var out []epmap.Entry
	for _, e := range series {
		if e.TVDBID > 0 && e.TVDBKind == "" && e.HasDefault {
			out = append(out, e)
		}
	}
	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool {
		a, b := out[i], out[j]
		if regular(a) != regular(b) {
			return regular(a)
		}
		if a.DefaultSeason != b.DefaultSeason {
			return a.DefaultSeason < b.DefaultSeason
		}
		if a.EpisodeOffset != b.EpisodeOffset {
			return a.EpisodeOffset < b.EpisodeOffset
		}
		return a.AniDBID < b.AniDBID
	})
	return out
}

func (p *Provider) seasons(ctx context.Context, a Anime) ([]metadata.SeasonRecord, int, error) {
	entry, ok, err := p.lists.Entry(ctx, a.ID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, 0, err
	}
	if !ok || entry.TVDBID == 0 {
		return standaloneSeasons(a), 0, nil
	}
	series, err := p.lists.EntriesByTVDB(ctx, entry.TVDBID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, 0, err
	}
	b := newSeasonBuilder()
	base, baseKnown := 0, true
	for _, e := range members(series) {
		an := a
		if e.AniDBID != a.ID {
			an, err = p.client.Anime(ctx, e.AniDBID)
			if err != nil {
				if ctx.Err() != nil {
					return nil, 0, ctx.Err()
				}
				p.log.Warn("anidb: series entry unavailable; later absolute numbers unknown", "aid", e.AniDBID, "err", err)
				if regular(e) && !e.Absolute {
					baseKnown = false
				}
				continue
			}
		}
		b.add(e, an, base, baseKnown)
		if regular(e) && !e.Absolute {
			if an.EpisodeCount > 0 {
				base += an.EpisodeCount
			} else {
				baseKnown = false
			}
		}
	}
	return b.seasons(), entry.TVDBID, nil
}

type seasonBuilder struct {
	eps map[int]map[int]metadata.EpisodeRecord
}

func newSeasonBuilder() *seasonBuilder {
	return &seasonBuilder{eps: map[int]map[int]metadata.EpisodeRecord{}}
}

func (b *seasonBuilder) put(r metadata.EpisodeRecord) {
	s := b.eps[r.Season]
	if s == nil {
		s = map[int]metadata.EpisodeRecord{}
		b.eps[r.Season] = s
	}
	if _, dup := s[r.Number]; !dup {
		s[r.Number] = r
	}
}

func (b *seasonBuilder) add(e epmap.Entry, an Anime, base int, baseKnown bool) {
	for _, ep := range an.Episodes {
		as, n, ok := ep.Key()
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		t, ok := e.ToTVDB(as, n)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		season := t.Season
		if season == epmap.SeasonAbsolute {
			season = 1 // absolute entry without a TVDB range: episodes keep absolute numbers
		}
		var abs *int
		if as == 1 && season >= 1 {
			switch {
			case e.Absolute:
				v := n + e.EpisodeOffset
				abs = &v
			case baseKnown && e.DefaultSeason >= 1:
				v := base + n
				abs = &v
			}
		}
		for _, num := range t.Episodes {
			b.put(metadata.EpisodeRecord{Season: season, Number: num, Absolute: abs,
				Title: ep.BestTitle(), Overview: strings.TrimSpace(ep.Summary), AirDate: strings.TrimSpace(ep.AirDate)})
		}
	}
}

func (b *seasonBuilder) seasons() []metadata.SeasonRecord {
	nums := make([]int, 0, len(b.eps))
	for n := range b.eps {
		nums = append(nums, n)
	}
	sort.Ints(nums)
	out := make([]metadata.SeasonRecord, 0, len(nums))
	for _, n := range nums {
		sr := metadata.SeasonRecord{Number: n, Title: fmt.Sprintf("Season %d", n)}
		if n == 0 {
			sr.Title = "Specials"
		}
		epNums := make([]int, 0, len(b.eps[n]))
		for en := range b.eps[n] {
			epNums = append(epNums, en)
		}
		sort.Ints(epNums)
		for _, en := range epNums {
			sr.Episodes = append(sr.Episodes, b.eps[n][en])
		}
		out = append(out, sr)
	}
	return out
}

// standaloneSeasons is the layout for anime without a TVDB series.
func standaloneSeasons(a Anime) []metadata.SeasonRecord {
	b := newSeasonBuilder()
	for _, ep := range a.Episodes {
		as, n, ok := ep.Key()
		if !ok || n > 200 {
			continue
		}
		r := metadata.EpisodeRecord{Season: 0, Number: n, Title: ep.BestTitle(),
			Overview: strings.TrimSpace(ep.Summary), AirDate: strings.TrimSpace(ep.AirDate)}
		if as == 1 {
			v := n
			r.Season, r.Absolute = 1, &v
		}
		b.put(r)
	}
	return b.seasons()
}
```

### Task 13: Setup: settings, registration, field priority, refresher, admin status

**Field priority for anime libraries** (provider names: `anilist`, `anidb`, `jikan` from 1E;
`tmdb`, `fanart` from 1D). `ids`, `ratings` and `alt_titles` are unions in 1D's merge, so they need
no entry.

| Field | Order | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `*` (title, original_title, overview, year, released, studios, …) | anilist, anidb, jikan, tmdb, fanart | spec 3.2: AniList primary for descriptive fields |
| `genres` | anilist, jikan, tmdb | AniDB has tags, not genres |
| `tags` | anilist, anidb | |
| `content_rating` | jikan, tmdb | only MAL has an anime rating usable for profile limits |
| `people` | tmdb | no anime provider supplies people in 1E |
| `seasons` | anidb, tmdb | AniDB episode lists and types; TMDB fallback |
| `images.poster` | anilist, tmdb, fanart, anidb, jikan | AniList art first |
| `images.banner` | anilist, fanart, tmdb | |
| `images.backdrop` | tmdb, fanart | spec: TMDB fallback for backdrops (AniList has none) |
| `images.logo`, `images.clearart` | fanart, tmdb | |
| `images.thumb` | tmdb, fanart | episode stills |

**Settings:** `LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT` / `LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER` (env wins), else
`server_settings` keys `anime.anidb.client` / `anime.anidb.clientver`; `LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM`
(1-90, default 30). An admin UI to edit the settings keys belongs to the admin dashboard (phase 3);
until then they are set by env or SQL.

**Admin status:** `GET /api/v1/anime/status` (admin only) →
`{"providers":[{"name":"anilist","enabled":true},{"name":"anidb","enabled":false,"reason":"AniDB needs a registered HTTP API client: …"},{"name":"jikan","enabled":true}],"datasets":[{"name":"anime-lists-ids","url":"…","fetched_at":"…","attempted_at":"…","rows":38250}]}`.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/metadata/anime/animesetup/settings.go`, `animesetup/priority.go`, `animesetup/setup.go`, `animesetup/module.go`
- Test: `server/internal/metadata/anime/animesetup/settings_test.go`, `animesetup/setup_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; `metadata.Registry` (via `Registrar`), `metadata.Field*`, `metadata.ImageField`,
  `metadata.Image*`, `config.Config.CacheDir`, `db.DB`, `authctx.Guard`, `authctx.StaticGuard`, `httpx`,
  `version.Version`, `epmap.Libraries` (`*library.Store`).
- Produces:
  - `animesetup.SettingAniDBClient = "anime.anidb.client"`, `SettingAniDBClientVer = "anime.anidb.clientver"`
  - `type animesetup.Settings struct{ AniDB anidb.Config; AniListRPM int }`; `animesetup.LoadSettings(ctx, d *db.DB, getenv func(string) string) (Settings, error)`
  - `type animesetup.Registrar interface{ Register(metadata.Provider); SetIDMapper(metadata.IDMapper); SetEpisodeMapper(metadata.EpisodeMapper); SetPriority(lt library.Type, field string, providers ...string) }` (`*metadata.Registry` satisfies it)
  - `animesetup.Priorities []Priority` (`type Priority struct{ Field string; Providers []string }`), `animesetup.ApplyPriorities(r Registrar)`
  - `type animesetup.URLs struct{ AniList, Jikan, AniDB, AniDBTitles, ListsMaster, ListsFull string }` (empty = production)
  - `type animesetup.Deps struct{ Config config.Config; DB *db.DB; Log *slog.Logger; Registry Registrar; Libraries epmap.Libraries; Getenv func(string) string; HTTP *http.Client; URLs URLs; StartDelay, RefreshEvery time.Duration }`
  - `animesetup.Setup(ctx, d Deps) (*Module, error)`: registers `anilist`, `anidb` (only when its client is valid), `jikan` in that order; sets ID and episode mappers and priorities; starts the dataset refresher (first run after `StartDelay`, default 30 s; then every `RefreshEvery`, default 6 h) until ctx ends.
  - `(*Module)` implements `app.Module` (`Name() == "anime"`, mounts `/anime/status`); `(*Module).Refresh(ctx)` runs one refresh pass (datasets + cache prune).

```go
package animesetup

import (
	"context"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
)

func envOf(m map[string]string) func(string) string { return func(k string) string { return m[k] } }

func TestLoadSettings(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	d := dbtest.Open(t)

	s, err := LoadSettings(ctx, d, envOf(nil))
	if err != nil || s.AniDB.Client != "" || s.AniListRPM != 30 {
		t.Fatalf("defaults: %+v %v", s, err)
	}

	d.SetSetting(ctx, SettingAniDBClient, " fromdb ")
	d.SetSetting(ctx, SettingAniDBClientVer, "3")
	s, _ = LoadSettings(ctx, d, envOf(nil))
	if s.AniDB.Client != "fromdb" || s.AniDB.ClientVer != "3" {
		t.Fatalf("settings table: %+v", s.AniDB)
	}

	s, _ = LoadSettings(ctx, d, envOf(map[string]string{
		"LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT": "fromenv", "LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER": "7", "LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM": "90",
	}))
	if s.AniDB.Client != "fromenv" || s.AniDB.ClientVer != "7" || s.AniListRPM != 90 {
		t.Fatalf("env must win: %+v", s)
	}

	for _, bad := range []string{"0", "91", "fast"} {
		_, err := LoadSettings(ctx, d, envOf(map[string]string{"LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM": bad}))
		if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM") {
			t.Errorf("RPM %q: err %v", bad, err)
		}
	}
}
```
