```go
package animesetup

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/app"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/authctx"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/config"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db/dbtest"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists"
)

var (
	_ Registrar  = (*metadata.Registry)(nil)
	_ app.Module = (*Module)(nil)
)

type fakeRegistrar struct {
	providers []string
	idMapper  metadata.IDMapper
	epMapper  metadata.EpisodeMapper
	prio      map[string][]string
	libTypes  map[library.Type]bool
}

func (f *fakeRegistrar) Register(p metadata.Provider)             { f.providers = append(f.providers, p.Name()) }
func (f *fakeRegistrar) SetIDMapper(m metadata.IDMapper)           { f.idMapper = m }
func (f *fakeRegistrar) SetEpisodeMapper(m metadata.EpisodeMapper) { f.epMapper = m }
func (f *fakeRegistrar) SetPriority(lt library.Type, field string, providers ...string) {
	if f.prio == nil {
		f.prio, f.libTypes = map[string][]string{}, map[library.Type]bool{}
	}
	f.prio[field] = providers
	f.libTypes[lt] = true
}

// fixtureServer serves the anime-lists fixtures and fails the test on any other request.
func fixtureServer(t *testing.T) *httptest.Server {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc("/master.xml", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		http.ServeFile(w, r, "../animelists/testdata/anime-list-master.xml")
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("/full.json", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		http.ServeFile(w, r, "../animelists/testdata/anime-list-full.json")
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("/titles.xml.gz", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		http.ServeFile(w, r, "../anidb/testdata/anime-titles.xml")
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("/", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		t.Errorf("unexpected request %s", r.URL.Path)
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusTeapot)
	})
	srv := httptest.NewServer(mux)
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	return srv
}

func testDeps(t *testing.T, reg Registrar, env map[string]string) Deps {
	srv := fixtureServer(t)
	return Deps{
		Config:   config.Config{CacheDir: t.TempDir()},
		DB:       dbtest.Open(t),
		Log:      slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)),
		Registry: reg,
		Getenv:   envOf(env),
		URLs: URLs{
			AniList: srv.URL + "/anilist", Jikan: srv.URL + "/jikan", AniDB: srv.URL + "/anidb",
			AniDBTitles: srv.URL + "/titles.xml.gz", ListsMaster: srv.URL + "/master.xml", ListsFull: srv.URL + "/full.json",
		},
		StartDelay: time.Hour,
	}
}

func statusOf(t *testing.T, m *Module, admin bool) (int, map[string]any) {
	t.Helper()
	r := chi.NewRouter()
	m.Mount(r, authctx.StaticGuard{P: authctx.Principal{UserID: 1, IsAdmin: admin}})
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	r.ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/anime/status", nil))
	var body map[string]any
	json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body)
	return rec.Code, body
}

func TestSetupWithoutAniDBClient(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	reg := &fakeRegistrar{}
	m, err := Setup(ctx, testDeps(t, reg, nil))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(reg.providers, []string{"anilist", "jikan"}) {
		t.Fatalf("registered %v", reg.providers)
	}
	if _, ok := reg.idMapper.(*animelists.Store); !ok || reg.epMapper == nil {
		t.Fatalf("mappers not set: %T %T", reg.idMapper, reg.epMapper)
	}
	if len(reg.libTypes) != 1 || !reg.libTypes[anime.LibAnime] {
		t.Fatalf("priorities must only touch anime libraries: %v", reg.libTypes)
	}
	if reg.prio["seasons"][0] != "anidb" || reg.prio["*"][0] != "anilist" ||
		reg.prio["content_rating"][0] != "jikan" || reg.prio["images.backdrop"][0] != "tmdb" {
		t.Fatalf("priorities %v", reg.prio)
	}

	code, body := statusOf(t, m, true)
	if code != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("status code %d", code)
	}
	provs := body["providers"].([]any)
	if len(provs) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("providers %v", provs)
	}
	adb := provs[1].(map[string]any)
	if adb["name"] != "anidb" || adb["enabled"] != false || !strings.Contains(adb["reason"].(string), "LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT") {
		t.Fatalf("anidb status %v", adb)
	}
	if ds := body["datasets"].([]any); len(ds) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("no dataset should be loaded before the first refresh: %v", ds)
	}
	if code, _ := statusOf(t, m, false); code != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("non-admin got %d", code)
	}
	if m.Name() != "anime" {
		t.Fatal("module name")
	}
}

func TestSetupWithAniDBClientAndRefresh(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	reg := &fakeRegistrar{}
	deps := testDeps(t, reg, map[string]string{"LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT": "lisimflixtest", "LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER": "1"})
	m, err := Setup(ctx, deps)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(reg.providers, []string{"anilist", "anidb", "jikan"}) {
		t.Fatalf("registered %v", reg.providers)
	}

	m.Refresh(ctx)
	lists := animelists.NewStore(deps.DB)
	if e, ok, _ := lists.Entry(ctx, 14444); !ok || e.EpisodeOffset != 12 {
		t.Fatal("anime-list-master not loaded by Refresh")
	}
	got, err := lists.Expand(ctx, map[string]string{"anilist": "104578"})
	if err != nil || got["anidb"] != "14444" {
		t.Fatalf("ids not loaded: %v %v", got, err)
	}
	_, body := statusOf(t, m, true)
	ds := body["datasets"].([]any)
	if len(ds) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("datasets %v", ds)
	}
	for _, d := range ds {
		if d.(map[string]any)["rows"].(float64) <= 0 {
			t.Fatalf("dataset without rows: %v", d)
		}
	}
}

func TestSetupWithRealRegistry(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	reg := metadata.NewRegistry()
	if _, err := Setup(ctx, testDeps(t, reg, nil)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := reg.Provider("anilist"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("anilist not registered in the real registry")
	}
	if _, ok := reg.CurrentIDMapper().(*animelists.Store); !ok {
		t.Fatalf("id mapper %T", reg.CurrentIDMapper())
	}
}

func TestSetupRejectsBadSettings(t *testing.T) {
	if _, err := Setup(context.Background(), testDeps(t, &fakeRegistrar{}, map[string]string{"LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM": "500"})); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected error")
	}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animesetup/settings.go`:
```go
// Package animesetup wires the anime metadata providers (plan 1E) into the
// server: settings, registry registration, field priorities, dataset refresh
// and the admin status endpoint.
package animesetup

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb"
)

const (
	SettingAniDBClient    = "anime.anidb.client"
	SettingAniDBClientVer = "anime.anidb.clientver"
	defaultAniListRPM     = 30
)

// Settings are the anime provider settings.
type Settings struct {
	AniDB      anidb.Config
	AniListRPM int
}

// LoadSettings reads env first, then server_settings.
func LoadSettings(ctx context.Context, d *db.DB, getenv func(string) string) (Settings, error) {
	get := func(env, key string) (string, error) {
		if v := strings.TrimSpace(getenv(env)); v != "" {
			return v, nil
		}
		v, _, err := d.GetSetting(ctx, key)
		return strings.TrimSpace(v), err
	}
	client, err := get("LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENT", SettingAniDBClient)
	if err != nil {
		return Settings{}, err
	}
	ver, err := get("LISIMFLIX_ANIDB_CLIENTVER", SettingAniDBClientVer)
	if err != nil {
		return Settings{}, err
	}
	rpm := defaultAniListRPM
	if v := strings.TrimSpace(getenv("LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM")); v != "" {
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
		if err != nil || n < 1 || n > 90 {
			return Settings{}, fmt.Errorf("LISIMFLIX_ANILIST_RPM must be a number from 1 to 90, got %q", v)
		}
		rpm = n
	}
	return Settings{AniDB: anidb.Config{Client: client, ClientVer: ver}, AniListRPM: rpm}, nil
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animesetup/priority.go`:
```go
package animesetup

import (
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/library"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
)

// Registrar is the part of *metadata.Registry that 1E uses.
type Registrar interface {
	Register(p metadata.Provider)
	SetIDMapper(m metadata.IDMapper)
	SetEpisodeMapper(m metadata.EpisodeMapper)
	SetPriority(lt library.Type, field string, providers ...string)
}

// Priority is the provider order of one field.
type Priority struct {
	Field     string
	Providers []string
}

// Priorities for anime libraries (plan 1E Task 13 table).
var Priorities = []Priority{
	{"*", []string{"anilist", "anidb", "jikan", "tmdb", "fanart"}},
	{metadata.FieldGenres, []string{"anilist", "jikan", "tmdb"}},
	{metadata.FieldTags, []string{"anilist", "anidb"}},
	{metadata.FieldContentRating, []string{"jikan", "tmdb"}},
	{metadata.FieldPeople, []string{"tmdb"}},
	{metadata.FieldSeasons, []string{"anidb", "tmdb"}},
	{metadata.ImageField(metadata.ImagePoster), []string{"anilist", "tmdb", "fanart", "anidb", "jikan"}},
	{metadata.ImageField(metadata.ImageBanner), []string{"anilist", "fanart", "tmdb"}},
	{metadata.ImageField(metadata.ImageBackdrop), []string{"tmdb", "fanart"}},
	{metadata.ImageField(metadata.ImageLogo), []string{"fanart", "tmdb"}},
	{metadata.ImageField(metadata.ImageClearArt), []string{"fanart", "tmdb"}},
	{metadata.ImageField(metadata.ImageThumb), []string{"tmdb", "fanart"}},
}

// ApplyPriorities sets Priorities for anime libraries only.
func ApplyPriorities(r Registrar) {
	for _, p := range Priorities {
		r.SetPriority(anime.LibAnime, p.Field, p.Providers...)
	}
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animesetup/module.go`:
```go
package animesetup

import (
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/authctx"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/httpx"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
)

type statuser interface{ Status() anime.Status }

// Module is the anime app.Module: admin status plus the dataset refresher.
type Module struct {
	providers []statuser
	datasets  *animecache.Datasets
	sets      []animecache.Dataset
	cache     *animecache.Cache
	log       *slog.Logger
}

func (m *Module) Name() string { return "anime" }

// Mount adds GET /api/v1/anime/status (admin only).
func (m *Module) Mount(r chi.Router, g authctx.Guard) {
	r.Route("/anime", func(r chi.Router) {
		r.With(g.RequireAdmin).Get("/status", m.status)
	})
}

type statusResponse struct {
	Providers []anime.Status              `json:"providers"`
	Datasets  []animecache.DatasetState `json:"datasets"`
}

func (m *Module) status(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	ds, err := m.datasets.All(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		m.log.Error("anime status: read datasets", "err", err)
		httpx.WriteError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal", "could not read anime dataset state")
		return
	}
	out := statusResponse{Providers: []anime.Status{}, Datasets: ds}
	if out.Datasets == nil {
		out.Datasets = []animecache.DatasetState{}
	}
	for _, p := range m.providers {
		out.Providers = append(out.Providers, p.Status())
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, out)
}
```

`server/internal/metadata/anime/animesetup/setup.go`:
```go
package animesetup

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/config"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/db"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/anidb"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/anilist"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animecache"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/animelists"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/epmap"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/httpc"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/metadata/anime/jikan"
	"github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix/server/internal/version"
)

// URLs overrides the API and dataset locations (tests). Empty = production.
type URLs struct {
	AniList, Jikan, AniDB, AniDBTitles, ListsMaster, ListsFull string
}

func (u URLs) withDefaults() URLs {
	def := func(v, d string) string {
		if v == "" {
			return d
		}
		return v
	}
	return URLs{
		AniList:     def(u.AniList, anilist.DefaultEndpoint),
		Jikan:       def(u.Jikan, jikan.DefaultBaseURL),
		AniDB:       def(u.AniDB, anidb.DefaultBaseURL),
		AniDBTitles: def(u.AniDBTitles, anidb.DefaultTitlesURL),
		ListsMaster: def(u.ListsMaster, animelists.MasterURL),
		ListsFull:   def(u.ListsFull, animelists.FullURL),
	}
}

// Deps are Setup's inputs. Registry and DB are required.
type Deps struct {
	Config       config.Config
	DB           *db.DB
	Log          *slog.Logger
	Registry     Registrar
	Libraries    epmap.Libraries // *library.Store
	Getenv       func(string) string
	HTTP         *http.Client // nil = 60 s timeout client
	URLs         URLs
	StartDelay   time.Duration // before the first dataset refresh; default 30 s
	RefreshEvery time.Duration // default 6 h
}

// Setup registers the anime providers and starts the dataset refresher.
func Setup(ctx context.Context, d Deps) (*Module, error) {
	if d.Log == nil {
		d.Log = slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	}
	if d.Getenv == nil {
		d.Getenv = func(string) string { return "" }
	}
	st, err := LoadSettings(ctx, d.DB, d.Getenv)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	u := d.URLs.withDefaults()
	ua := "lisimflix/" + version.Version + " (+https://github.com/Lisimflix/lisimflix)"
	newClient := func(interval time.Duration) *httpc.Client {
		return httpc.New(d.HTTP, httpc.NewLimiter(interval), ua)
	}

	cache := animecache.New(d.DB)
	lists := animelists.NewStore(d.DB)
	ds := &animecache.Datasets{DB: d.DB, Client: newClient(time.Second),
		Dir: filepath.Join(d.Config.CacheDir, "anime"), Now: time.Now, Log: d.Log}
	sets := lists.Datasets(u.ListsMaster, u.ListsFull)

	al := anilist.New(u.AniList, newClient(time.Minute/time.Duration(st.AniListRPM)), cache)
	jk := jikan.New(u.Jikan, newClient(jikan.DefaultInterval), cache)
	adCfg := st.AniDB
	adCfg.BaseURL, adCfg.TitlesURL = u.AniDB, u.AniDBTitles
	adCfg = adCfg.WithDefaults()
	adClient := anidb.NewClient(ctx, adCfg, newClient(adCfg.Interval), cache, d.DB, time.Now)
	titles := anidb.NewTitleIndex(d.DB)
	ad := anidb.NewProvider(adClient, titles, lists, d.Log)

	d.Registry.Register(al)
	if adClient.Enabled() {
		d.Registry.Register(ad)
		sets = append(sets, titles.Dataset(adCfg.TitlesURL))
	} else {
		d.Log.Warn("anidb provider disabled", "reason", adClient.Status().Reason)
	}
	d.Registry.Register(jk)
	d.Registry.SetIDMapper(lists)
	d.Registry.SetEpisodeMapper(epmap.NewMapper(lists, d.Libraries, d.Log))
	ApplyPriorities(d.Registry)

	m := &Module{providers: []statuser{al, ad, jk}, datasets: ds, sets: sets, cache: cache, log: d.Log}
	delay, every := d.StartDelay, d.RefreshEvery
	if delay <= 0 {
		delay = 30 * time.Second
	}
	if every <= 0 {
		every = 6 * time.Hour
	}
	go m.refreshLoop(ctx, delay, every)
	return m, nil
}

// Refresh runs one pass: every dataset's Ensure, then a cache prune.
func (m *Module) Refresh(ctx context.Context) {
	for _, s := range m.sets {
		loaded, err := m.datasets.Ensure(ctx, s)
		switch {
		case err != nil:
			m.log.Warn("anime dataset refresh failed", "dataset", s.Name, "err", err)
		case loaded:
			m.log.Info("anime dataset loaded", "dataset", s.Name)
		}
	}
	if n, err := m.cache.Prune(ctx, 30*24*time.Hour); err != nil {
		m.log.Warn("anime cache prune failed", "err", err)
	} else if n > 0 {
		m.log.Debug("anime cache pruned", "rows", n)
	}
}

func (m *Module) refreshLoop(ctx context.Context, delay, every time.Duration) {
	t := time.NewTimer(delay)
	defer t.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-t.C:
		}
		m.Refresh(ctx)
		t.Reset(every)
	}
}
```

and-run check against 1D's real `Registry`; if it fails to compile, 1D's `SetPriority` signature
changed and only `priority.go` needs adjusting.
